## Shipping

Follow the Nexo flow in `AGENTS.md`.
Keep changes atomic, verify locally, capture the run under `nexo/`, commit with a Conventional Commit message, and push `master` after Gate 2 passes.

<!-- nexo:managed:start version=4 sha256=02fd055249adb37ac71ec0db87e5e313c575eadc762d972ac81c07a1a398a84b -->
## Nexo workflow contract

Nexo owns the delivery workflow while a Nexo flow is active.
Work moves through Frame, Plan, Execute, Verify, and Capture.
Feature and batch flows plan the complete initial slice set before execution, then adapt only within the finite runtime policy.

The human owns WHAT and why.
The agent owns HOW.
Gate 1 is human approval of WHAT and is skipped only by explicit autopilot.
Gate 2 is local verification and is never skipped.
Gate 3 is the human-approved release cut and is never automatic.

Verification is tiered.
Each slice runs its named locked oracle tests plus lint on changed files.
Each integrated wave runs the full suite, full lint, and security checks once.
Each feature runs mutation testing once after all waves are green.
Execute and Verify use separate agents whenever the host supports them and the user has not explicitly required single-agent execution.

Delivery is local trunk flow.
Verified short-lived branches merge serially to `main` with no pull request and no hosted CI requirement.
Promotion to `staging` and `production` exists only when `nexo/state.json` opts into it, and every promotion is fast-forward-only.
The user never commits by hand because Nexo owns branch, commit, verification, merge, and cleanup.

Autopilot never waits for a human and never expands a budget.
A blocker or exhausted budget is recorded in `AUDIT.md`, unfinished work is parked, owned worktrees and processes are cleaned up, and the run returns a partial completion report.
<!-- nexo:managed:end -->
# Prompt — FXL Vendas: CRM unificado (v1)

> Rodar no FXL Harness (Claude Code), na pasta do repo
> `/Users/cauetpinciara/Documents/fxl/projects/06--PRODUCT--fxl-sales`, com
> `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-empresa`. Base de contexto: `.nexo/tmp/vendas-modos-analise.md`.

---

/nexo-feature --autopilot

> Modo autopilot (padrão Nexo): sem Gate 1 humano dentro da run; bloqueios viram `AUDIT.md` + pendência e a run segue. Gate 2 (Verify separado) nunca é pulado.

## 0. Regras invioláveis desta run (ler antes de tudo)

1. **Nada de produção.** Sem SSH, túnel, banco real, dados reais de cliente, `make update-app`, `git push`, deploy, Coolify, Vercel, Hub de produção. Trunk local é **`master`** (ver `nexo/state.json` → `delivery.trunk`); o merge acontece só no `master` LOCAL. O repo está em `delivery.mode: "promotion"`, mas **esta run nunca chama `/nexo-ship` nem `/nexo-ship-prod-ready`** — produção só depois, via `/nexo-ship`, com OK explícito do Cauê em outra sessão.
2. **Worktrees** em `<repo>/.worktrees/<run-id>/...` via `nexo-worktree.sh` (flows/feature.md). **Nunca apagar branches nem worktrees** — nem os desta run, nem os existentes. Onde o fluxo Nexo mandaria limpar (cleanup do `#### 9 · Report`, "delete branch"), **pular** e registrar no relatório como "mantido por ordem do Cauê".
3. **Modelos explícitos por papel, nunca `inherit`:**
   | Papel | Modelo | Faz |
   |---|---|---|
   | Orquestrador (host) | **Opus 5.5** | Frame, plano abstrato de fases, gates, merge. **Não implementa código.** |
   | Planner / plan-checker / Verify (Gate 2) / scribe | **`sonnet`** (versão atual) | detalha cada fase em slices; Verify separado de quem implementou |
   | Executor | **`haiku`** (versão atual) | implementa Red→Green→Refactor |
   | Reviewer (code review por wave) | **Codex CLI `-m gpt-6-astra high`** (explícito; o padrão global `gpt-6.1-sol medium` NÃO vale aqui) | papel `reviewer` do Nexo (`agents/reviewer.md`), via `python3 "$NEXO_SCRIPTS/nexo-agent.py" run reviewer --repo <worktree> --run "$RUN_ID" --slice <slice>` (B2 em `flows/feature.md`); roda pela **assinatura do Codex (login ChatGPT), nunca por API key** |
   Todo spawn passa `--model` / `model:` explícito e grava `--agent-model` no status (engine.md `## Model tiers`, "The host model never reaches a leg"). Todo comando `claude` roda com `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-empresa`. Sem API paga direta.
4. **Testes de migração só com dados isolados**: Postgres local/efêmero do `docker-compose.yml`/test harness (`apps/api/test/`), fixtures sintéticas. O guard `scripts/__tests__/local-database-guard.test.mjs` deve continuar passando.
5. **Ids técnicos não mudam**: `fxlAppId`/`name` `fxl-sales` (package.json raiz, `packages/auth-fake/src/index.ts:99`), pacotes `@fxl-sales/*`, domínios `sales.fxlbusiness.com` / `sales-api.fxlbusiness.com` (`apps/api/src/middleware/app-auth.ts:~434`), envs `SALES_*`, eventos `fxl-sales.obligation.upserted|settlement.recorded|settlement.reversed` (`apps/api/src/domains/integration/events.ts`), chaves de localStorage `fxl-sales.*` (`apps/web/src/auth/session-recovery.ts`, `apps/web/src/dev/*`). Tabelas `sales_ops_*` e identificadores `salesOps*` também ficam.
6. **Fases pequenas**, cada uma com Verify (Gate 2) e testes. Se algo exigir produção ou dado real, **não fazer**: registrar como pendência.
7. O relatório final **confirma o modelo efetivamente usado em cada papel** (lido dos result files / status, não presumido).

## 1. Contexto (o que existe hoje — confirme relendo o código)

- Um app, duas "edições": `SalesEdition = 'full' | 'leads'` em `packages/shared-utils/src/sales-edition.ts`; derivada por request do token do Hub (`entitlements.modules` contém `sales.edition.leads`) em `apps/api/src/middleware/app-auth.ts:276`; gate de capability em `apps/api/src/middleware/require-capability.ts`. Edição `leads` = Construbom (só `clients` + `import`).
- Funil: tabela `sales_ops_lead_stages` (migr. `apps/api/drizzle/0022_sales_ops_leads.sql`; kinds `normal|conversion|lost`, `is_system`, índice único parcial por kind). Seeds `LEAD_STAGE_SEEDS` + `ensureLeadStages`/`ensureLeadStagesForOrg` em `apps/api/src/domains/sales-ops/leads/stages-seed.ts`. Leitura `listLeadStages` em `stage-service.ts:79` **não semeia**; único caller do seed em runtime é `apps/api/src/domains/import/routes.ts:52`. Por isso orgs criadas após a 0022 (ECV Control) ficam sem etapas.
- Lead do CRM: `sales_ops_leads` (+ `sales_ops_lead_products`; campos de contato da migr. 0027; soft delete 0028; `sale_id` liga à proposta). Dois contratos (`leadFieldSet`: `full`/`contact`) em `apps/api/src/domains/sales-ops/leads/lead-schemas.ts`. Serviços: `lead-service.ts`, `lead-trash-service.ts`, rotas `lead-routes.ts`, `stage-routes.ts`.
- Lead legado de indicação: tabela `leads` (`apps/api/src/db/schema.ts:233`, finder/click/link), usada em `apps/api/src/domains/conversions/service.ts`.
- Propostas/vendas: `sales_ops_sales` (`status` `draft|open|won|lost|cancelled`, schema.ts ~l.809), `_sale_items`, `_sale_professionals`; financeiro: `sales_ops_receivables`, `sales_ops_payables`, `sales_ops_settlements`; integração com Finanças por outbox (`domains/integration/*`, eventos `fxl-sales.*`).
- Web: rota única `/:workspace/:view/:saleId?` em `apps/web/src/sales-ops/navigation.ts` (listas paralelas `leadsEdition*`); `apps/web/src/sales-ops/SalesOpsApp.tsx` tem ~9.700 linhas; kanban em `apps/web/src/sales-ops/leads/*`; settlements em `apps/web/src/sales-ops/settlements/*`; importação em `apps/web/src/sales-ops/import` + `apps/api/src/domains/import/*`.
- Marca na UI: `apps/web/src/sales-ops/missing-entitlement-copy.ts`, `apps/web/src/i18n/{pt-BR,en}.json`, `apps/api/src/domains/import/template.ts`, `apps/web/index.html` (título), comentários.

## 2. Base aprovada pelo Cauê (o QUÊ)

1. **Marca:** "FXL Sales" → **"FXL Vendas"** em tudo que o usuário vê (UI, i18n, planilha modelo, título, e-mails/textos). Ids técnicos inalterados (regra 0.5).
2. **Um app só, sem edições.** Remover `full`/`leads` como conceito. Módulos por organização **no banco**: plano + feature flags (ex.: `orcamentos`, `comissoes`, `financeiro`, `finders`, `catalogo`, `importacao`). Substitui `sales.edition.leads` do token. Construbom = uma org com `orcamentos` (e dependentes) desligados. Transição: enquanto existir org com o módulo antigo no token, um backfill/compat lê o token uma vez e grava a flag (documentar; não tocar no Hub de produção).
3. **Funil como fluxograma:**
   - etapa com **tipo + comportamento** (ex.: `orcamento` abre o módulo Orçamentos; `normal`; `saida`);
   - **transições permitidas** explícitas (grafo);
   - **etapas de saída tipadas**: `ganho`, `perdido`, `pausado`;
   - **vários funis por organização** (ex.: "Novos", "Carteira");
   - **Kanban é só uma visualização** do funil;
   - org sem configuração recebe o **funil CRM padrão** (semeado de forma idempotente).
4. **Lead:** obrigatórios só **nome** e **contato**. O resto são **campos dinâmicos tipados e filtráveis**: SIMPLES (texto, número, valor, data, opções) ou REFERÊNCIA (Cliente, Contato, Produto, Serviço, Venda). **Cliente** é campo de referência padrão, **não removível**.
5. **Página do lead:** clicar abre **resumo** (não o form): etapa atual, histórico com notas, botão **Avançar** listando só as transições válidas. Ao avançar, pedir **próximo passo + data** → lista de **follow-ups**. Histórico de mudança de etapa gravado **automaticamente** (servidor).
6. **Migração sem perda:** nada de leads, etapas ou dados do modo full se perde. Tabelas `leads` (legado finder) e `sales_ops_leads` **unificadas se o levantamento permitir** (risco alto ⇒ pendência), com **teste de migração** (contagens, somas de valor, vínculos `sale_id`, soft-deletados, seller, client, stage, produtos).
7. **Componente único de lead/prospecção:** criar cliente/empresa ao digitar o nome (hoje só na edição leads: picker "Buscar ou criar novo cliente"); ao escolher a empresa, **contatos e responsável** vêm preenchidos.
8. **Proposta x vendido** separados pelo **tipo da etapa**. Todas as listas e o Kanban com **filtro por período**. Cada subitem do menu Propostas vira **lista própria** (rota própria).
9. **Bug ECV:** chamar `ensureLeadStagesForOrg` ao carregar (bootstrap e/ou `GET /lead-stages`).
10. **Segunda leva:** "dias sem comprar" (a partir da última venda ganha) e importação de planilha com **mapeamento de colunas → campos e etapas**.
11. **Fora do escopo (só listar como futuro no relatório/ROADMAP):** onboarding (configurar funis, campos, módulos), agente de IA, conexão de agenda.

## 3. Processo

- O Orquestrador (Opus 5.5) escreve um **plano abstrato** (`nexo/runs/<run-id>/00-OVERVIEW.md`) com as fases abaixo, dependências e riscos. Não escreve código.
- Para **cada fase**: Planner `sonnet` detalha slices (arquivos, testes oráculo, critérios) → plan-checker `sonnet` → Executor `haiku` em worktree `feat/<token>-<slice>` → **Verify `sonnet` separado** (oráculo + suíte afetada + lint + type-check; por wave: `pnpm test`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm type-check`, `pnpm build`, integração com Postgres local) → **Code review Codex** (papel `reviewer` do Nexo, `-m gpt-6-astra high`, pela assinatura; se `agents.enabled` não estiver ligado no repo, rodar o mesmo papel com `codex exec -m gpt-6-astra` e reasoning `high` no worktree, só leitura) → **achado bloqueante ⇒ volta ao Executor `haiku`, refaz Verify `sonnet` e refaz o review** (dentro dos limites de rounds do `runtime-policy.json`; esgotou ⇒ `AUDIT.md` + pendência, sem merge) → merge `--no-ff` no `master` local → Capture (`nexo/runs/`, `nexo/knowledge/`).
- Uma fase só começa quando a anterior está verde no `master` local.
- Bloqueio que precise do Cauê ou de produção → `AUDIT.md` + pendência no relatório; seguir com o que não depende disso.
- **Migrações 100% aditivas (regra dura):** Drizzle em `apps/api/drizzle/` (próximo número após `0028_*`). **Zero `DROP`** (tabela, coluna, índice, constraint) que remova ou deixe de proteger dados, **zero `DELETE`/`TRUNCATE` de dados**, **nenhum rename destrutivo** (`RENAME`, `ALTER COLUMN ... TYPE` com perda). Só `CREATE`, `ADD COLUMN` (nullable ou com default), `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, `UPDATE` de backfill em colunas novas. Se um CHECK ou índice atual bloquear o modelo novo (ex.: 0022 — `sales_ops_lead_stages_org_kind_idx`, uma `conversion` e uma `lost` por org; `sales_ops_lead_stages_system_kind_check`), a solução tem de ser aditiva (tabelas/colunas novas onde a regra antiga não se aplica, ex.: etapas de funil novas em tabela própria, com a tabela antiga intacta) — senão **vira pendência**. Um teste/guard da wave varre as migrações novas e falha se encontrar `DROP`, `DELETE`, `TRUNCATE` ou `RENAME`. Respeitar o contrato single-role (sem GRANT/ROLE em migração) e RLS por `org_id` (`FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` + policies tenant/admin como na 0022).

## 4. Fases e critérios de aceite

> **AC comum a todas as fases:** cada wave só faz merge com Verify `sonnet` verde **e** review Codex (`gpt-6-astra high`) sem achado bloqueante, ambos registrados no status/run.

### Fase 0 — Marca, bug ECV, funil padrão (pequena, primeiro)
- 0a Marca: todo texto visível "FXL Sales" → "FXL Vendas" (`missing-entitlement-copy.ts`, `i18n/*.json`, `import/template.ts` incl. `wb.creator`, `apps/web/index.html`). **AC:** grep de "FXL Sales" em `apps/web/src` + strings de UI da API = 0 (comentários podem ficar); `fxl-sales` ids intactos (teste guarda contando appId/eventos/envs/localStorage inalterados); testes de copy atualizados.
- 0b Bug ECV: `GET /lead-stages` e o bootstrap chamam `ensureLeadStagesForOrg`. **AC:** teste de integração: org nova sem etapas → após o GET tem as 4 seeds; segunda chamada não duplica; org que renomeou/arquivou etapas não recebe reinserção; concorrência (2 GETs simultâneos) não gera 500.
- 0c Funil padrão: definir o "funil CRM padrão" (proposta inicial: Novo → Qualificação → Em negociação → Proposta(orçamento) → Ganho / Perdido / Pausado) **como constante única**, ainda sobre o modelo atual se possível. **AC:** fonte única da seed, teste que compara constante e migração (como `leads-schema-migration.test.ts`).

### Fase 1 — Levantamento das entidades (documento obrigatório + Verify) → modelo de dados e migração
- **1a Levantamento crítico (só leitura, entrega um documento `nexo/runs/<run-id>/levantamento-entidades.md`)** para cada entidade: Lead (`sales_ops_leads` e `leads` legado), Etapa, Funil, Cliente/Empresa (`sales_ops_clients`), Contato (hoje campos do lead), Pessoa/Vendedor/Responsável (`sales_ops_people`, funções), Produto/Serviço (`sales_ops_products`, áreas, custos por função), Proposta/Orçamento/Venda (`sales_ops_sales`, itens, profissionais), Recebível/A pagar/Liquidação, Comissão/Finder/Indicação/Click/Conversão, Importação, Auditoria/histórico. Para cada uma: onde aparece (arquivos API e web), telas, rotas, eventos e outbox (**incluindo o que o FXL Finanças consome** via `fxl-sales.obligation.*`/`settlement.*`), RLS, testes; **o que quebra se virar campo dinâmico**; recomendação **fixo vs configurável**. **Documento obrigatório**, conferido por um **Verify `sonnet` separado** (confere cada citação contra o código e a classificação de risco) antes de qualquer modelo de dados. Cada entidade recebe risco **baixo/médio/alto**. **Risco alto ⇒ caminho conservador:** não unificar, não mudar, registrar como pendência no relatório. São risco alto por definição: unificar `leads` (legado finder) com `sales_ops_leads`; qualquer mudança nos eventos `fxl-sales.obligation.*`/`settlement.*`; a pagar e a receber (`sales_ops_payables`, `sales_ops_receivables`, `sales_ops_settlements`). Nada de modelo novo antes do Verify do levantamento passar.
- **1b Modelo de dados** (após o Verify do levantamento; só para entidades de risco baixo/médio): funis (`pipelines`), etapas com `type`/`behavior`/`exit_kind`, transições, definições de campos por org (tipo simples/referência, filtrável), valores de campos (estratégia de armazenamento e índice para filtro — justificar JSONB vs EAV), lead unificado, eventos/histórico de etapa, notas, follow-ups (próximo passo + data), módulos/flags por org. **AC:** ADR em `nexo/knowledge/decisions/` citando o levantamento; schema Drizzle + migração 100% aditiva com RLS; guard de migração aditiva verde; entidades de risco alto sem alteração e listadas como pendência.
- **1c Migração de dados** (cópia aditiva; origens intactas): backfill de `sales_ops_leads` para o lead novo — `leads` (legado finder) **só entra se o levantamento não a classificar como risco alto** (por definição é; então fica como pendência, sem tocar); etapas atuais → funil "Padrão" de cada org (`conversion` → tipo orçamento/proposta; `lost` → saída perdido); `stage_changed_at` vira primeiro evento de histórico; Cliente vira valor do campo de referência; produtos do lead viram campo referência Produto. **AC:** teste de migração com dataset sintético isolado cobrindo os dois modos (org full com propostas ganhas, perdidas, leads convertidos, soft-deletados, sem vendedor; org estilo Construbom sem produtos; org sem etapas; leads legados de finder com click/link/conversion) — **todas as tabelas de origem com contagem e conteúdo idênticos antes/depois** (nada apagado/alterado), contagens e somas do destino batendo com a origem, payables/receivables/settlements e outbox inalterados, nenhum FK órfão, RLS isolando orgs, rodar migração 2x é idempotente. Leitura antiga continua funcionando (compat) até a Fase 2.

### Fase 2 — Componente e página do lead
- Componente único de criação/edição (substitui os dois contratos `full`/`contact`): criar empresa ao digitar; ao escolher empresa preenche contatos e responsável; campos dinâmicos renderizados por tipo.
- Página de resumo do lead (nova rota, ex.: `/operacional/leads/:leadId`): etapa atual, histórico + notas, botão Avançar com só as transições válidas, modal de próximo passo + data; lista de follow-ups (minha/da equipe, filtro por período).
- Kanban vira visualização do funil selecionado (seletor de funil); Proposta x Vendido separados pelo tipo da etapa; filtro por período em todas as listas e no Kanban; cada subitem do menu Propostas com rota/lista própria (atualizar `navigation.ts`, sem listas paralelas por edição).
- **AC:** testes de componente (web vitest) e de API: transição inválida → 4xx; avançar grava evento de histórico no servidor; follow-up obrigatório com data; Cliente não removível; filtros por período corretos com fuso America/Sao_Paulo; clicar no card abre o resumo, não o form.

### Fase 3 — Módulos por organização
- Tabela de plano/flags por org; `require-capability.ts` passa a ler do banco (cache por request); `resolveSalesEdition` e `SALES_EDITION_LEADS_MODULE` deixam de decidir produto (manter compat de leitura do token só para backfill inicial, documentado). Remover `leadsEdition*` de `navigation.ts` em favor de filtro por módulo. Etapa tipo orçamento só aparece/age se módulo Orçamentos ativo.
- **AC:** testes: org com orçamentos off (Construbom) vê exatamente o que vê hoje + novidades da Fase 2; org full sem regressão (suíte inteira verde); toggle de flag muda navegação e 403 `edition_capability` (ou novo código equivalente, documentado) sem redeploy; nenhum teste de guard (`no-legacy-*`, `hub-sdk-pin`, `fxl-contracts-pin`) quebrado.

### Fase 4 — Segunda leva
- "Dias sem comprar" por cliente = hoje − data da última venda **ganha** (etapa de saída ganho / `sales_ops_sales.status='won'`), exibido e filtrável. **AC:** teste com cliente sem venda (vazio, não 0), com várias vendas, com venda perdida ignorada.
- Importação com mapeamento de colunas → campos dinâmicos e etapas/funis (evolui `apps/api/src/domains/import/*` e `apps/web/src/sales-ops/import`), com preview. **AC:** preview + commit idempotentes; reconhecimento de cliente existente mantido; planilha modelo atualizada com marca FXL Vendas.

## 5. Relatório final (obrigatório)
1. O que entrou por fase, commits no `master` local, worktrees/branches criados (todos mantidos).
2. **Modelo efetivo por papel** (orquestrador, planner, plan-checker, executor, verify, reviewer Codex, scribe), lido dos registros. **Confirmar, wave por wave, que o review do Codex rodou**, com o modelo (`gpt-6-astra high`), o modo de autenticação (assinatura), o veredito e os rounds de correção.
3. Resultado de cada Gate 2 / wave-verify (números de testes).
4. **Pendências:** entidades de risco alto não alteradas (com o motivo do levantamento), soluções aditivas não encontradas; **dados reais:** impacto da migração em produção (volumes, tempo, ordem web/API, rollback), backfill das flags a partir do Hub, eventual limpeza futura de tabelas/colunas antigas (só com decisão do Cauê), ativação/validação com Construbom e ECV Control — tudo **não executado**.
5. Futuro (não feito): onboarding de funis/campos/módulos, agente de IA, conexão de agenda.
6. Lembrete: produção só via `/nexo-ship` com OK explícito do Cauê.

+/**
+ * The projection of an `orcamento` stage for ONE organization (per-org modules, phase 3).
+ * A stage opens a proposta only while `orcamentos` is on; with it off the stage is an
+ * ordinary stage in every projection. The STORED `type` and `behavior` are never rewritten,
+ * so turning the module back on restores the stage exactly.
+ */
+export function legacyStageKindFor(stage: KindInput & { behavior: string }, modules: OrgModules): LegacyStageKind {
+  const acts = stageActsAsOrcamento({ type: stage.type, behavior: stage.behavior }, modules);
+  return legacyStageKind({ type: acts ? stage.type : 'normal', exitKind: stage.exitKind });
+}
+
+/** `GET /lead-stages` row as this organization's modules let the stage act. */
+export function leadStageViewFor(view: LeadStageView, modules: OrgModules): LeadStageView {
+  const kind = legacyStageKindFor(view, modules);
+  return {
+    ...view,
+    kind,
+    isSystem: kind !== 'normal',
+    behavior: effectiveStageBehavior({ type: view.type, behavior: view.behavior }, modules),
+  };
+}
diff --git a/apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/context.ts b/apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/context.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5fa3b45
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/context.ts

+/**
+ * The organization's modules for THIS request: one query, memoized on the Hono context.
+ * Services never call this; routes resolve it and pass the value down as an argument.
+ */
+import {
+  ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
+  editionFromModules,
+  type OrgModules,
+} from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
+import type { SalesEdition } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition';
+import type { Context } from 'hono';
+import { loadOrgModules } from './service.js';
+
+const KEY = 'orgModules';
+
+type Loose = { get(key: string): unknown; set(key: string, value: unknown): void };
+
+/**
+ * A value already on the context wins (tests inject it; the first call stores it).
+ * A context with neither modules nor a verified Hub context is the whole product -
+ * the same fail-to-full rule requireCapability always had. A store failure THROWS.
+ */
+export async function getOrgModules(c: Context): Promise<OrgModules> {
+  const loose = c as unknown as Loose;
+  const preset = loose.get(KEY) as OrgModules | undefined;
+  if (preset) return preset;
+  const hubAuth = loose.get('hubAuth') as { entitlements?: { modules?: readonly unknown[] } } | undefined;
+  const orgId = loose.get('orgId') as string | undefined;
+  if (!hubAuth || !orgId) return ALL_MODULES_ENABLED;
+  const state = await loadOrgModules(orgId, hubAuth.entitlements?.modules);
+  loose.set(KEY, state.modules);
+  return state.modules;
+}
+
+/** The legacy two-value projection of the request's modules (orcamentos off => 'leads'). */
+export async function getSalesEdition(c: Context): Promise<SalesEdition> {
+  return editionFromModules(await getOrgModules(c));
+}
diff --git a/apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/service.ts b/apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/service.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e99217e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/service.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,120 @@
+/**
+ * The per-organization module flags (`sales_ops_org_modules`).
+ *
+ * The token's `entitlements.modules` is consulted ONLY here, and only for an
+ * organization with no row at all: that one-time backfill reproduces the edition the
+ * organization had before modules existed. After it, the database decides.
+ */
+import {
+  CRM_MODULE_KEYS,
+  CRM_MODULE_SOURCES,
+  isCrmModuleKey,
+  modulesForEdition,
+  type CrmModuleKey,
+  type CrmModuleSource,
+  type OrgModules,
+} from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
+import { resolveSalesEdition } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition';
+import { eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
+import { getDb } from '../../db/client.js';
+import { salesOpsOrgModules } from '../../db/schema.js';
+import { withTenant } from '../sales-ops/leads/with-tenant.js';
+
+type Db = ReturnType<typeof getDb>;
+
+export type OrgModuleSources = Record<CrmModuleKey, CrmModuleSource>;
+export interface OrgModulesState {
+  modules: OrgModules;
+  sources: OrgModuleSources;
+}
+export type OrgModulesPatch = Partial<Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>>;
+
+type ModuleRow = typeof salesOpsOrgModules.$inferSelect;
+
+function isModuleSource(value: string): value is CrmModuleSource {
+  return (CRM_MODULE_SOURCES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
+}
+
+function stateFromRows(rows: readonly ModuleRow[]): OrgModulesState {
+  const modules = {} as OrgModules;
+  const sources = {} as OrgModuleSources;
+  for (const key of CRM_MODULE_KEYS) {
+    modules[key] = false;
+    sources[key] = 'default';
+  }
+  for (const row of rows) {
+    if (!isCrmModuleKey(row.moduleKey)) continue;
+    modules[row.moduleKey] = row.enabled;
+    sources[row.moduleKey] = isModuleSource(row.source) ? row.source : 'default';
+  }
+  return { modules, sources };
+}
+
+async function readRows(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<ModuleRow[]> {
+  return tx.select().from(salesOpsOrgModules).where(eq(salesOpsOrgModules.orgId, orgId));
+}
+
+/**
+ * Returns the org's rows, writing the one-time compat backfill first when it has none.
+ * MUST run inside a tenant transaction.
+ */
+async function ensureRows(
+  tx: Db,
+  orgId: string,
+  tokenModules: readonly unknown[] | null | undefined,
+): Promise<ModuleRow[]> {
+  const existing = await readRows(tx, orgId);
+  if (existing.length > 0) return existing;
+
+  // Serialize first backfills of the same org: the loser blocks here, then re-reads.
+  await tx.execute(sql`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${`org-modules:${orgId}`}, 0))`);
+  const again = await readRows(tx, orgId);
+  if (again.length > 0) return again;
+
+  const seed = modulesForEdition(resolveSalesEdition(tokenModules));
+  await tx
+    .insert(salesOpsOrgModules)
+    .values(
+      CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((moduleKey) => ({
+        orgId,
+        moduleKey,
+        enabled: seed[moduleKey],
+        source: 'token_backfill' as const,
+      })),
+    )
+    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: [salesOpsOrgModules.orgId, salesOpsOrgModules.moduleKey] });
+  return readRows(tx, orgId);
+}
+
+/** The org's modules, backfilled from the token on first sight. One transaction. */
+export async function loadOrgModules(
+  orgId: string,
+  tokenModules: readonly unknown[] | null | undefined,
+  db: Db = getDb(),
+): Promise<OrgModulesState> {
+  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => stateFromRows(await ensureRows(tx, orgId, tokenModules)));
+}
+
+/** Admin toggle: ensure the backfill, then upsert the patched keys as source 'admin'. */
+export async function updateOrgModules(
+  orgId: string,
+  tokenModules: readonly unknown[] | null | undefined,
+  patch: OrgModulesPatch,
+  db: Db = getDb(),
+): Promise<OrgModulesState> {
+  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
+    await ensureRows(tx, orgId, tokenModules);
+    for (const key of CRM_MODULE_KEYS) {
+      const enabled = patch[key];
+      if (enabled === undefined) continue;
+      await tx
+        .insert(salesOpsOrgModules)
+        .values({ orgId, moduleKey: key, enabled, source: 'admin', updatedAt: new Date() })
+        .onConflictDoUpdate({
+          target: [salesOpsOrgModules.orgId, salesOpsOrgModules.moduleKey],
+          set: { enabled, source: 'admin', updatedAt: new Date() },
+        });
+    }
+    return stateFromRows(await readRows(tx, orgId));
+  });
+}

+import type { MiddlewareHandler } from 'hono';
+import { env } from '../env.js';
+
+/**
+ * The 403 an organization's module write answers to an admin who is not a platform
+ * operator. Distinct from `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY` so a log line can tell "you lack
+ * the tenant admin role" from "your account may not change modules".
+ */
+export const MODULE_OPERATOR_REQUIRED_BODY = { error: 'forbidden', code: 'module_operator_required' } as const;
+
+/**
+ * The platform-operator allow-list, parsed from the validated env on every call. Empty
+ * or absent means nobody: default deny.
+ */
+export function moduleOperatorAccountIds(
+  raw: string | undefined = env.SALES_MODULE_OPERATOR_ACCOUNT_IDS,
+): ReadonlySet<string> {
+  return new Set(
+    (raw ?? '')
+      .split(',')
+      .map((id) => id.trim())
+      .filter((id) => id !== ''),
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * A tenant admin cannot enable a module for their own organization: a module write is a
+ * platform-operator action. It runs AFTER `requireAdmin`, and it reads only the verified
+ * account id (`c.get('userId')`), never the body and never the org.
+ */
+export const requireModuleOperator: MiddlewareHandler = async (c, next) => {
+  if (!moduleOperatorAccountIds().has(c.get('userId'))) {
+    return c.json(MODULE_OPERATOR_REQUIRED_BODY, 403);
+  }
+  return next();
+};

# 11a - Org modules: pure vocabulary and the loader (phase 3, API core)

Phase 3 plan group: 11a (this), 11b (cutover of the gates), 11c (admin route, bootstrap, orcamento stage rule, docs), 12a/12b (web).
Why split: the owner asked for one slice `11-api-modulos-org`, but it needs ~17 files and touches 9 locked oracles; 11a is dormant additive code (no behaviour change), 11b is the risky switch, 11c is the surface. The orchestrator may rename; the dependency chain is 11a -> 11b -> 11c -> 12b and 11a -> 12a.

## Context

- `SalesEdition = 'full' | 'leads'`, `SalesCapability`, `hasCapability`, `resolveSalesEdition`, `SALES_EDITION_LEADS_MODULE` live in `packages/shared-utils/src/sales-edition.ts`. They are NOT edited.
- Slice 05b created `packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts` with `CRM_MODULE_KEYS` (`orcamentos|comissoes|financeiro|finders|catalogo|importacao|clientes`), `CrmModuleKey`, `CRM_MODULE_SOURCES`, `CrmModuleSource`, `isCrmModuleKey`, `CAPABILITY_MODULES`, `modulesForEdition`, `StageBehavior`, `defaultStageBehavior`. Read the file first: `grep -n "^export" packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts`.
- Slice 05a created `sales_ops_org_modules` (Drizzle export `salesOpsOrgModules` in `apps/api/src/db/schema.ts`): `id`, `orgId`, `moduleKey`, `enabled` (default false), `source` (default 'default'), `createdAt`, `updatedAt`; unique index `sales_ops_org_modules_org_key_idx (org_id, module_key)`; FORCE RLS with `sales_ops_org_modules_tenant_isolation` and `sales_ops_org_modules_admin_context`. Nobody writes it before this slice.
- Equivalence facts (verified from `CAPABILITY_MODULES` and `LEADS_CAPABILITIES`): `modulesForEdition('leads')` enables exactly `clientes` and `importacao`; `modulesForEdition('full')` enables all seven. Capability `history` maps to `financeiro`; `leadFullFields` and `catalog` map to `catalogo`.
- Tenant door: `withTenant(db, orgId, fn)` re-exported by `apps/api/src/domains/sales-ops/leads/with-tenant.ts` (opens a transaction and sets `app.current_org_id`).
- Integration harness to copy: `apps/api/test/rls/leads-edition.test.ts` lines 1-60 and 80-140 (`testDatabaseUrls()`, `postgres(APP_DB_URL)` for the RLS-live app role, `postgres(ADMIN_DB_URL, {connection: {'app.fxl_admin': 'true'}})` for setup and teardown). Token fixture: `hubAuthContext({ entitlements: { modules: [...] } })` from `apps/api/src/auth/__tests__/hub-auth-context-fixture.ts` is NOT needed here: the loader takes the token's module list as a plain argument.

## Design (decided)

1. The module set of an org lives ONLY in `sales_ops_org_modules`. A row per `CRM_MODULE_KEYS` entry. A key with no row reads as `false`. A row whose `module_key` is not a known key is ignored. An unknown `source` reads as `'default'`.
2. One-time backfill. `loadOrgModules(orgId, tokenModules)` reads the org's rows in a tenant transaction. If there are NONE it takes a per-org transaction advisory lock, re-reads (a concurrent winner may have committed), and only if still empty inserts the 7 rows from `modulesForEdition(resolveSalesEdition(tokenModules))` with `source = 'token_backfill'` using `ON CONFLICT (org_id, module_key) DO NOTHING`. After that the token is never consulted again for that org. An org with at least one row is never backfilled (a key added to `CRM_MODULE_KEYS` later ships its own additive `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` migration).
3. The advisory lock makes the backfill all-or-nothing even when two concurrent requests carry different tokens (the second blocks, re-reads, finds 7 rows, inserts nothing). The unique index plus `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` is the second line of defence.
4. `updateOrgModules(orgId, tokenModules, patch)` runs the same ensure step first (so an admin write on a never-seen org cannot skip the compat backfill), then upserts each patched key with `source = 'admin'` and `updated_at = now()`.
5. A store failure throws; callers never treat it as "all enabled" (fail closed, a 500 from the app `onError`).
6. Pure additions to `crm-model.ts` (shared by API and web): `OrgModules`, `ALL_MODULES_ENABLED`, `modulesAllowCapability`, `editionFromModules`, `isOrgModules`, `stageActsAsOrcamento`, `effectiveStageBehavior`. `editionFromModules(modules)` is `modules.orcamentos ? 'full' : 'leads'` - the derived PROJECTION that keeps every existing `edition` argument working: "orcamentos off behaves like today's leads edition".
7. `context.ts` gives the request-level API: `getOrgModules(c)` (memoized on the Hono context under the key `orgModules`; a value already on the context wins, which is the test seam), `getSalesEdition(c)` (the projection). A context with neither `orgModules` nor `hubAuth` (hand-built test context; impossible behind `appAuthMiddleware`) is `ALL_MODULES_ENABLED`, the same fail-to-full rule `requireCapability` has today, so this can never take a capability away from an unmarked org. Declaring the `orgModules` context variable type is done in slice 11b (`middleware/auth.ts`), which is why `context.ts` here uses a local typed accessor (see code).

## Steps (TDD)

1. Append the failing tests to `packages/shared-utils/src/__tests__/crm-model.test.ts` (text in Tests). Run: expect FAIL (exports missing).
2. Append the pure block to `packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts` (below). Build shared-utils. Re-run: PASS.
3. Create `apps/api/test/rls/org-modules.test.ts` (Tests). Run with `VITEST_INTEGRATION=1`: expect FAIL (service missing).
4. Create `apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/service.ts` and `context.ts` (code below). Re-run integration test: PASS. Run it 3 times in a row.
5. Run the focused and affected commands, lint, type-check.

### Code: append to `packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts`

The file already imports `hasCapability`, `SalesCapability`, `SalesEdition` from `./sales-edition.js`. Add nothing to the imports except, if `StageBehavior` is not already in scope, it is declared in this same file by 05b. Append at the end:

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Phase 3 (per-organization modules). Pure; shared by the API and the web.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** The module flags of one organization. Every key is present. */
export type OrgModules = Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>;

/** The fail-to-full value: a context that never resolved modules is the whole product. */
export const ALL_MODULES_ENABLED: Readonly<OrgModules> = Object.freeze(
  Object.fromEntries(CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((key) => [key, true])) as OrgModules,
);

/** A capability is granted iff EVERY module mapped to it is enabled. */
export function modulesAllowCapability(modules: OrgModules, capability: SalesCapability): boolean {
  return CAPABILITY_MODULES[capability].every((key) => modules[key] === true);
}

/**
 * The legacy two-value projection. 'full' while `orcamentos` is on, 'leads' while it
 * is off. It exists so every service argument and web conditional that still takes an
 * edition keeps working; it never decides a capability (modulesAllowCapability does).
 */
export function editionFromModules(modules: OrgModules): SalesEdition {
  return modules.orcamentos === true ? 'full' : 'leads';
}

/** True only for an object with every module key present as a boolean. */
export function isOrgModules(value: unknown): value is OrgModules {
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  return CRM_MODULE_KEYS.every((key) => typeof record[key] === 'boolean');
}

/**
 * An `orcamento` stage with the `opens_orcamentos` behaviour opens the proposta
 * wizard / answers sale_required_for_conversion ONLY while the organization has the
 * `orcamentos` module. With it off the stage is an ordinary stage.
 */
export function stageActsAsOrcamento(
  stage: { type: string; behavior: string },
  modules: OrgModules,
): boolean {
  return modules.orcamentos === true && stage.type === 'orcamento' && stage.behavior === 'opens_orcamentos';
}

/** The behaviour a stage really has for this organization (`none` when it cannot act). */
export function effectiveStageBehavior(
  stage: { type: string; behavior: string },
  modules: OrgModules,
): StageBehavior {
  return stageActsAsOrcamento(stage, modules) ? 'opens_orcamentos' : 'none';
}
```

### Code: `apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/service.ts` (new, complete)

```ts
import {
  CRM_MODULE_KEYS,
  CRM_MODULE_SOURCES,
  isCrmModuleKey,
  modulesForEdition,
  type CrmModuleKey,
  type CrmModuleSource,
  type OrgModules,
} from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
import { resolveSalesEdition } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition';
import { and, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getDb } from '../../db/client.js';
import { salesOpsOrgModules } from '../../db/schema.js';
import { withTenant } from '../sales-ops/leads/with-tenant.js';

type Db = ReturnType<typeof getDb>;

export type OrgModuleSources = Record<CrmModuleKey, CrmModuleSource>;
export interface OrgModulesState {
  modules: OrgModules;
  sources: OrgModuleSources;
}
export type OrgModulesPatch = Partial<Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>>;

type ModuleRow = typeof salesOpsOrgModules.$inferSelect;

function isModuleSource(value: string): value is CrmModuleSource {
  return (CRM_MODULE_SOURCES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

function stateFromRows(rows: readonly ModuleRow[]): OrgModulesState {
  const modules = {} as OrgModules;
  const sources = {} as OrgModuleSources;
  for (const key of CRM_MODULE_KEYS) {
    modules[key] = false;
    sources[key] = 'default';
  }
  for (const row of rows) {
    if (!isCrmModuleKey(row.moduleKey)) continue;
    modules[row.moduleKey] = row.enabled;
    sources[row.moduleKey] = isModuleSource(row.source) ? row.source : 'default';
  }
  return { modules, sources };
}

async function readRows(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<ModuleRow[]> {
  return tx.select().from(salesOpsOrgModules).where(eq(salesOpsOrgModules.orgId, orgId));
}

/**
 * Returns the org's rows, writing the one-time compat backfill first when it has none.
 * MUST run inside a tenant transaction. The token decides ONLY here, and only for an
 * org with no row at all.
 */
async function ensureRows(
  tx: Db,
  orgId: string,
  tokenModules: readonly unknown[] | null | undefined,
): Promise<ModuleRow[]> {
  const existing = await readRows(tx, orgId);
  if (existing.length > 0) return existing;

  // Serialize first backfills of the same org: the loser blocks here, then re-reads.
  await tx.execute(sql`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${`org-modules:${orgId}`}, 0))`);
  const again = await readRows(tx, orgId);
  if (again.length > 0) return again;

  const seed = modulesForEdition(resolveSalesEdition(tokenModules));
  await tx
    .insert(salesOpsOrgModules)
    .values(
      CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((moduleKey) => ({
        orgId,
        moduleKey,
        enabled: seed[moduleKey],
        source: 'token_backfill' as const,
      })),
    )
    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: [salesOpsOrgModules.orgId, salesOpsOrgModules.moduleKey] });
  return readRows(tx, orgId);
}

/** The org's modules, backfilled from the token on first sight. One transaction. */
export async function loadOrgModules(
  orgId: string,
  tokenModules: readonly unknown[] | null | undefined,
  db: Db = getDb(),
): Promise<OrgModulesState> {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => stateFromRows(await ensureRows(tx, orgId, tokenModules)));
}

/** Admin toggle: ensure the backfill, then upsert the patched keys as source 'admin'. */
export async function updateOrgModules(
  orgId: string,
  tokenModules: readonly unknown[] | null | undefined,
  patch: OrgModulesPatch,
  db: Db = getDb(),
): Promise<OrgModulesState> {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
    await ensureRows(tx, orgId, tokenModules);
    for (const key of CRM_MODULE_KEYS) {
      const enabled = patch[key];
      if (enabled === undefined) continue;
      await tx
        .insert(salesOpsOrgModules)
        .values({ orgId, moduleKey: key, enabled, source: 'admin', updatedAt: new Date() })
        .onConflictDoUpdate({
          target: [salesOpsOrgModules.orgId, salesOpsOrgModules.moduleKey],
          set: { enabled, source: 'admin', updatedAt: new Date() },
        });
    }
    return stateFromRows(await readRows(tx, orgId));
  });
}
```

(`and` is imported only if needed; remove unused imports so lint passes.)

### Code: `apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/context.ts` (new, complete)

```ts
import {
  ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
  editionFromModules,
  type OrgModules,
} from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
import type { SalesEdition } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition';
import type { Context } from 'hono';
import { loadOrgModules } from './service.js';

const KEY = 'orgModules';

type Loose = { get(key: string): unknown; set(key: string, value: unknown): void };

/**
 * The organization's modules for THIS request: one query, memoized on the context.
 * A value already on the context wins (tests inject it; the first call stores it).
 * A context with neither modules nor a verified Hub context is the whole product -
 * the same fail-to-full rule requireCapability always had. A store failure THROWS.
 */
export async function getOrgModules(c: Context): Promise<OrgModules> {
  const loose = c as unknown as Loose;
  const preset = loose.get(KEY) as OrgModules | undefined;
  if (preset) return preset;
  const hubAuth = loose.get('hubAuth') as { entitlements?: { modules?: readonly unknown[] } } | undefined;
  const orgId = loose.get('orgId') as string | undefined;
  if (!hubAuth || !orgId) return ALL_MODULES_ENABLED;
  const state = await loadOrgModules(orgId, hubAuth.entitlements?.modules);
  loose.set(KEY, state.modules);
  return state.modules;
}

/** The legacy two-value projection of the request's modules (orcamentos off => 'leads'). */
export async function getSalesEdition(c: Context): Promise<SalesEdition> {
  return editionFromModules(await getOrgModules(c));
}
```

(Slice 11b replaces the `Loose` cast with the typed `c.get('orgModules')` once `ContextVariableMap` declares it; 11a stays type-clean without touching `middleware/auth.ts`.)

## Tests

`packages/shared-utils/src/__tests__/crm-model.test.ts` (append; import the new names from `../crm-model.js`, `hasCapability` and `SalesCapability` from `../sales-edition.js`):
- `describe('org modules (phase 3)')`:
  - `it('ALL_MODULES_ENABLED is frozen with every key true')`: `Object.isFrozen`, `CRM_MODULE_KEYS.every(k => ALL_MODULES_ENABLED[k] === true)`.
  - `it.each(['full','leads'])('modulesAllowCapability reproduces hasCapability for %s on every capability')`: for each capability in the literal list `['proposals','commissions','catalog','clients','import','finders','history','leadFullFields']`, `modulesAllowCapability(modulesForEdition(edition), capability) === hasCapability(edition, capability)`. (This is the parity oracle: the module table is exactly today's editions.)
  - `it('editionFromModules round-trips both editions')`: `editionFromModules(modulesForEdition('full')) === 'full'`, same for `'leads'`.
  - `it('editionFromModules follows orcamentos alone')`: `{...ALL_MODULES_ENABLED, orcamentos:false}` -> `'leads'`; `{...modulesForEdition('leads'), orcamentos:true}` -> `'full'`.
  - `it('isOrgModules accepts a full record and rejects a missing key or a non-boolean')`.
  - `it('stageActsAsOrcamento needs orcamentos on, type orcamento and behavior opens_orcamentos')`: the 2x2x2 table - only (on, 'orcamento', 'opens_orcamentos') is true; `effectiveStageBehavior` returns 'opens_orcamentos' for that one and 'none' for the rest.

`apps/api/test/rls/org-modules.test.ts` (new; harness copied from `leads-edition.test.ts`: `appClient` RLS role, `adminClient`, `db = drizzle(appClient, { schema })`, org ids `org_mod_<label>_<Date.now()>_<uuid8>`, `afterAll` deletes `DELETE FROM sales_ops_org_modules WHERE org_id = ...` through `adminClient` for every created org and ends the clients). Import `loadOrgModules`, `updateOrgModules` from `../../src/domains/org-modules/service.js`, `CRM_MODULE_KEYS`, `modulesForEdition` from `@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model`. Rows are read back with `adminClient\`SELECT module_key, enabled, source FROM sales_ops_org_modules WHERE org_id = ${orgId} ORDER BY module_key\``. Tests:
1. `a Construbom-like org (token module sales.edition.leads, no rows) backfills exactly today's leads capabilities`: `loadOrgModules(org, ['sales.edition.leads'], db)`; state.modules equals `modulesForEdition('leads')`; admin read shows 7 rows, every `source` `token_backfill`, enabled keys sorted equal `['clientes','importacao']`.
2. `a full org (modules []) backfills all seven enabled`.
3. `token near-misses and unrelated add-ons backfill full`: tokens `['sales.some-other-addon']`, `['SALES.EDITION.LEADS']`, `['sales.edition.leads ']`, `undefined`, `null` each on a fresh org -> all seven true. And `['sales.some-other-addon','sales.edition.leads']` -> leads set.
4. `after the backfill the token no longer decides`: first call leads token, second call on the same org with `[]` returns the leads set and the row count stays 7 (and `updated_at` null).
5. `twelve concurrent first calls with mixed tokens write one coherent set`: `await Promise.all` of 12 `loadOrgModules` (even index leads token, odd index `[]`) on one fresh org; assert no rejection, exactly 7 rows, all 12 returned states deep-equal, the 7 rows have identical `source` `token_backfill`, and the winning set is either entirely the leads set or entirely the full set (never mixed: enabled count is 2 or 7).
6. `RLS isolates orgs`: after orgs A and B are loaded, `withTenant(db, B, tx => tx.select().from(salesOpsOrgModules))` returns only rows with `orgId === B` and `withTenant(db, B, tx => tx.select().from(salesOpsOrgModules).where(eq(salesOpsOrgModules.orgId, A)))` returns `[]`.
7. `updateOrgModules turns a flag on as source admin and leaves the rest`: leads org, `{ orcamentos: true }` -> orcamentos true, `sources.orcamentos === 'admin'`, `sources.clientes === 'token_backfill'`, other flags unchanged, `updated_at` set for that row only.
8. `updateOrgModules on a never-seen org backfills first`: fresh org, token leads, patch `{ comissoes: true }` -> 7 rows, comissoes true/admin, clientes+importacao true/token_backfill, rest false.
9. `a partially populated org is not backfilled; a missing key reads false`: insert ONE row (`orcamentos`, enabled true, source 'admin') through `adminClient`; `loadOrgModules(org, [], db)` -> only orcamentos true, `SELECT count(*)` still 1.
10. `an unknown module_key row is ignored and an unknown source reads default`: insert rows `('zzz', true, 'admin')` and `('clientes', true, 'weird')` via adminClient (so the org has rows); state.modules has no `zzz` key, `sources.clientes === 'default'`.

## Risks

- `hashtextextended` needs PostgreSQL 11+ (the project runs 16). The advisory lock is transaction-scoped, released at commit, no cleanup.
- `drizzle` `onConflictDoNothing({ target: [...] })` with a unique INDEX (not constraint) works because the target columns match the unique index `sales_ops_org_modules_org_key_idx`.
- If 05b named a helper differently (for example `CAPABILITY_MODULES` as a function), read the file first and keep the NAMES in `contracts/SEAM-CONTRACT.md`; do not rename them here.

# 11b - Cutover: requireCapability and edition readers on per-org modules (phase 3, API)

## Context (verified against the tree before phase 2; RE-GREP after slice 10a/10b)

- `apps/api/src/middleware/require-capability.ts`: `requireCapability(capability)` reads `c.get('salesEdition') ?? 'full'` and `hasCapability(edition, capability)`; body `EDITION_CAPABILITY_BODY = { error: 'forbidden', code: 'edition_capability' }`.
- `apps/api/src/middleware/app-auth.ts:13` imports `resolveSalesEdition`; `applyHubAuthContext` (~line 270-277) ends with `c.set('salesEdition', resolveSalesEdition(auth.entitlements?.modules));`. Its doc comment (lines ~253-263) describes the edition resolution.
- `apps/api/src/middleware/auth.ts:2,15` declares `salesEdition: SalesEdition` on `ContextVariableMap`.
- Readers of `c.get('salesEdition')`: `domains/sales-ops/routes.ts` (POST `/people` ~line 129, PATCH `/people/:id` ~line 145), `domains/sales-ops/leads/lead-routes.ts` (`leadScope` ~line 88, `usesContactFields` ~line 98, called from ~9 handlers). Phase 2 (10a) rewrites `lead-routes.ts`: re-run `grep -rn "salesEdition" apps/api/src | grep -v __tests__` and convert EVERY hit by the rule in Design 3.
- The gated routers: `salesOpsRouter.use('/summary/*' ... '/history/*', requireCapability(...))` (routes.ts lines 82-92) plus inline `PUT /settings`, and `commissions`, `payouts`, `payoutsAdmin`, `commissionsAdmin`, `links`, `finder` routers (`router.use('*', requireCapability(...))` as their first registration). They are NOT edited.
- Tests that set the edition by hand: `require-capability.test.ts:31,74`, `lead-routes-edition.test.ts:82`, `people-routes-edition.test.ts:66`, `import-routes.integration.test.ts:115` (`c.set('salesEdition', ...)`), probes in `app-auth.test.ts:182-214` and `dev-identity-no-hub.test.ts:73,230-271`.
- `edition-gate-map.test.ts` mocks `../../db/client.js` with a Proxy that throws on first use and asserts `dbCalls.count === 0` for every gated denial; it mounts the real `appAuthMiddleware` with real signed tokens whose `entitlements.modules` is `['sales.edition.leads']`, `[]` or `['sales.some-other-addon']`, workspace `org_edition_gate`.

## Design (decided)

1. `requireCapability(capability)`: `const modules = await getOrgModules(c); if (!modulesAllowCapability(modules, capability)) return c.json(EDITION_CAPABILITY_BODY, 403); return next();`. The code `edition_capability` and the body are KEPT byte for byte (the web and the logs key on it); its meaning becomes "the organization's modules do not include this".
2. `applyHubAuthContext` stops resolving anything about the edition. The token's `entitlements.modules` is read by exactly one place: `loadOrgModules` (the backfill). `resolveSalesEdition` is still exported from shared-utils and used by the backfill only.
3. Readers: every `c.get('salesEdition') ?? 'full'` becomes `await getSalesEdition(c)` (the projection `orcamentos off => 'leads'`). A synchronous helper that read it (`leadScope`, `usesContactFields`) becomes `async` and its call sites `await` it; pass the awaited value down exactly as before - no service reads the context.
4. `ContextVariableMap` drops `salesEdition` and gains `orgModules: OrgModules` (set lazily by `getOrgModules`, or by a test).
5. One query per request: the first `getOrgModules(c)` memoizes on the context; later gates and readers reuse it.
6. `edition-gate-map.test.ts` keeps the Proxy DB (so a handler reached is still detectable) and adds ONE module mock for the loader that reproduces what the real loader returns for a never-seen org: `modulesForEdition(resolveSalesEdition(tokenModules))`. Gated denials still cost zero DB calls; this is the "update the source of truth, never weaken" rule.

## Steps (TDD)

1. Edit the tests first (Tests below). Run them: `require-capability`, `app-auth`, `dev-identity` and the three set-by-hand tests fail to compile/run against the old code (expected RED).
2. Production edits:
   a. `middleware/auth.ts`: replace the import `import type { SalesEdition } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition';` with `import type { OrgModules } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';` and the `salesEdition: SalesEdition;` member (with its doc comment) by:
      ```ts
      /**
       * The ACTIVE organization's module flags, loaded lazily (one query per request) by
       * `getOrgModules` in `domains/org-modules/context.ts` from `sales_ops_org_modules`.
       * Read it through `requireCapability` or `getSalesEdition`, never from the token.
       */
      orgModules: OrgModules;
      ```
   b. `middleware/app-auth.ts`: delete the import line 13 and the `c.set('salesEdition', ...)` line; rewrite the paragraph of the `applyHubAuthContext` doc comment that says "It is also the ONE place the Sales edition is resolved ..." to: "It resolves NO edition and NO modules. `entitlements.modules` is read only by `loadOrgModules` (domains/org-modules/service.ts), once per organization, to seed `sales_ops_org_modules`; after that the database decides. Baseline access is still `entitlements.access`, decided by `requireHubAuth` before this function runs."
   c. `domains/org-modules/context.ts`: replace the `Loose` accessor by the typed one: `const preset = c.get('orgModules'); if (preset) return preset;` ... `c.set('orgModules', state.modules);` and read `c.get('hubAuth')` / `c.get('orgId')` typed. Delete `Loose` and `KEY`.
   d. `middleware/require-capability.ts`: replace the body per Design 1; imports `modulesAllowCapability` from `@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model` and `SalesCapability` type from `@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition`, `getOrgModules` from `../domains/org-modules/context.js`. Replace the doc comment's last paragraph ("What it narrows is the product surface of the organization's edition...") with: "What it narrows is the product surface of the organization's MODULES, read from `sales_ops_org_modules` through `getOrgModules` (one query per request, backfilled once from the token for an org with no rows). A context that never loaded modules and carries no Hub context is all modules, so this gate can never take a capability away from an unmarked org."
   e. `domains/sales-ops/routes.ts`: add `import { getSalesEdition } from '../org-modules/context.js';` and in the two people handlers replace `const edition = c.get('salesEdition') ?? 'full';` by `const edition = await getSalesEdition(c);`. Update the comment block above the gates (lines ~70-78): "The capability set per org lives in `sales_ops_org_modules`; `CAPABILITY_MODULES` in `@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model` maps each capability to its modules."
   f. `domains/sales-ops/leads/lead-routes.ts` (and any other hit of the grep): make `leadScope` `async` (`const edition = await getSalesEdition(c);` then the same object), `usesContactFields` `async`, and `await` them at every call site (`const scope = await leadScope(c)`; `leadScope(c).isAdmin` becomes `(await leadScope(c)).isAdmin`). Keep every service call otherwise byte-identical.
3. Run RED tests, now GREEN. Then the whole API unit suite: `pnpm --filter @fxl-sales/api test`. For any other unit test that mounts a router with a `hubAuth` on the context and now reaches the loader (and so a real `getDb()`), preset `c.set('orgModules', ALL_MODULES_ENABLED)` (or `modulesForEdition(...)`) in its hand-built middleware. Never mock `getDb` for this.
4. Run integration import test, lint, type-check, guards.

## Tests

- `require-capability.test.ts`: change `appFor(edition, capability)` so the middleware does `if (edition !== undefined) c.set('orgModules', modulesForEdition(edition))` (import `modulesForEdition` from `@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model`) and the second hand-built context at line ~74 likewise. Every existing case keeps its name and assertion (byte-exact body, leads refused 6 capabilities and granted `clients`/`import`, full passes all, missing edition treated as full). Add:
  - `it('a toggled flag changes the answer: leads modules plus comissoes passes commissions')`: `{...modulesForEdition('leads'), comissoes: true}` -> 200 for `commissions`, 403 for `proposals`.
  - `it('a capability needing two modules is refused when one is off')`: no such capability exists today, so assert instead `{...ALL_MODULES_ENABLED, catalogo:false}` refuses `catalog` and `leadFullFields` and passes `proposals`.
  - `it('loads the modules once per request even behind two gates')`: `vi.mock('../../domains/org-modules/service.js', () => ({ loadOrgModules: vi.fn(async () => ({ modules: ALL_MODULES_ENABLED, sources: {} })) }))` (use `vi.hoisted` for the shared fn); a Hono app with a middleware setting `hubAuth = { entitlements: { modules: [] } }` and `orgId = 'org_x'`, then `app.get('/p', requireCapability('proposals'), requireCapability('clients'), handler)`; request once; `loadOrgModules` toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1).
  - `it('a store failure is not a denial and not a pass')`: `loadOrgModules` rejects; the request answers 500 (app `onError` returns 500) and the handler is not reached.
- `edition-gate-map.test.ts`: add after the existing `vi.mock('../../db/client.js', ...)`:
  ```ts
  vi.mock('../../domains/org-modules/service.js', async () => {
    const { CRM_MODULE_KEYS, modulesForEdition } = await import('@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model');
    const { resolveSalesEdition } = await import('@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition');
    return {
      // What the real loader returns for a never-seen org: the one-time token backfill.
      loadOrgModules: async (_orgId: string, tokenModules: readonly unknown[] | undefined) => {
        const modules = modulesForEdition(resolveSalesEdition(tokenModules));
        return {
          modules,
          sources: Object.fromEntries(CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((key) => [key, 'token_backfill'])),
        };
      },
      updateOrgModules: async () => {
        throw new Error('edition-gate-map: updateOrgModules reached');
      },
    };
  });
  ```
  Change the header comment's first paragraph by one sentence: "The module loader is mocked (it would otherwise be the first database use) with the token backfill it performs for a never-seen org." Do NOT touch GATED, OPEN, LEADS_GRANTED or any assertion.
- `app-auth.test.ts`: replace the `describe('applyHubAuthContext resolves the sales edition once', ...)` block (lines ~178-216) by `describe('applyHubAuthContext resolves no edition and no modules', ...)` with the same `probe` helper but the handler returns `{ orgModules: c.get('orgModules') ?? null, orgId: c.get('orgId') }`; cases: modules `[]`, `['sales.edition.leads']`, `['sales.some-other-addon']` and `undefined` each give `orgModules === null` (lazy: nothing loaded, no DB touched); `tenancy is unchanged` keeps `orgId === 'org_active_1'`. The token-to-edition table that used to live here is now covered by `test/rls/org-modules.test.ts` case 3 (11a).
- `dev-identity-no-hub.test.ts`: at the top add `vi.mock('../../domains/org-modules/service.js', async () => { ...same factory as the gate-map mock, loadOrgModules only... })` (ensure `vi` is imported from vitest). In the `/probe` handler (line ~64-75) make the callback `async (c) =>` and replace `salesEdition: c.get('salesEdition')` by `salesEdition: await getSalesEdition(c)` with `const { getSalesEdition } = await import('../../domains/org-modules/context.js');` resolved in `beforeAll` next to `appAuth`. Test names and assertions (`resolveSalesEdition(identity.modules)`, `'full'` for the roster, `'leads'` for the two leads identities) stay unchanged: they now prove the dev identity's module claim reaches the backfill.
- `lead-routes-edition.test.ts:82`, `people-routes-edition.test.ts:66`, `import-routes.integration.test.ts:115`: replace `c.set('salesEdition', X)` by `c.set('orgModules', modulesForEdition(X))` (add the import). The comment at the top of `lead-routes-edition.test.ts` that says "the `salesEdition` the auth middleware resolves from the VERIFIED token" becomes "the modules the loader resolves for the organization".

## Risks

- Latency: every authenticated sales-ops request now pays one extra tenant transaction. Accepted (the request asked for per-request, not cross-request, caching so a toggle needs no redeploy and no invalidation).
- A forgotten reader that still calls `c.get('salesEdition')` would be a TypeScript error after step 2a (the member is gone); that is the intended net.
- dev-fake: the `leads-owner`/`leads-seller` identities backfill into the LOCAL database on their first request; `make db-reset` clears it. Document in 11c.

```yaml
verifier_focus: "Run the integration route test 3 times; run edition-gate-map and the whole API suite; read the diff of CLAUDE.md to confirm only the listed paragraphs changed; confirm GET /org-modules for a non-admin answers the ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY"
oracle: "apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/__tests__/org-modules-routes.integration.test.ts"
```

# 11c - Admin toggle route, bootstrap exposure, orcamento stage rule, API docs (phase 3)

## Context

- After 11b the gates and readers use `getOrgModules` / `getSalesEdition` (`apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/context.ts`), the loader/updater are in `service.ts` (11a).
- `salesOpsRouter` (`apps/api/src/domains/sales-ops/routes.ts`) registers the capability gates at the top (lines ~82-92), then `GET /bootstrap` (~line 94: `ensureLeadStagesForOrg` or, after phase 2, `ensureCrmDefaultsForOrg`, then `getSalesOpsSnapshot`, then `return c.json(snapshot)`).
- `requireAdmin` and `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY` are in `apps/api/src/middleware/require-admin.ts`.
- Phase 2 contract (`contracts/SEAM-CONTRACT-PHASE2.md`): `GET /pipelines`, `GET /lead-stages` (legacy `kind` derived: `type 'orcamento'` -> `'conversion'`, `exit_kind 'perdido'` -> `'lost'`, else `'normal'`; additive `type`, `behavior`, `exitKind`), `POST /leads/:id/advance` and `POST /leads/:id/move` answer `409 { error: 'sale_required_for_conversion' }` for a destination of type `orcamento` with behaviour `opens_orcamentos`. Today (pre phase 2) that refusal is `LeadInputError('sale_required_for_conversion')` at `apps/api/src/domains/sales-ops/leads/lead-service.ts:1310` and the import executor message at `apps/api/src/domains/import/executor.ts:91`.
- The zod import in `routes.ts` already exists (`z`).

## Design (decided)

1. Routes (in `routes.ts`, registered after the gates and before `/bootstrap`, so the gate comment "above every route" still holds):
   - `GET /org-modules` (`requireAdmin`): `{ modules: Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>, sources: Record<CrmModuleKey, 'default'|'token_backfill'|'admin'> }` from `loadOrgModules(orgId, tokenModules)`.
   - `PUT /org-modules` (`requireAdmin`): body `{ modules: { <key>?: boolean, ... } }`, strict (an unknown key, a non-boolean or an empty `modules` is `400 { error: 'validation_error', issues }`), answers `200` with the same body as GET after `updateOrgModules`. Dependencies between modules are NOT enforced (turning `orcamentos` off while `comissoes` stays on is allowed; the screens simply have nothing to attach to). No audit entry in this run (see Risks).
   - `tokenModules` is `c.get('hubAuth')?.entitlements?.modules`.
   - Neither route is capability gated; both are added to `edition-gate-map.test.ts` `OPEN`.
2. Bootstrap: `GET /bootstrap` returns `c.json({ ...snapshot, orgModules: await getOrgModules(c) })`. This is the web's source of truth for navigation (slice 12b). Additive; every existing key is untouched.
3. Orcamento stage rule. Pure helpers `stageActsAsOrcamento(stage, modules)` and `effectiveStageBehavior(stage, modules)` exist (11a). Apply them at EVERY decision site, found with:
   `grep -rn "opens_orcamentos\|'orcamento'\|'conversion'\|sale_required_for_conversion" apps/api/src --include='*.ts' | grep -v __tests__`
   Rules per hit kind:
   - A refusal or branch that makes an `orcamento`/`opens_orcamentos` destination require a sale (`sale_required_for_conversion`, move or advance): wrap the condition with `stageActsAsOrcamento(stage, modules)`. When it is false the stage is a normal stage: the move/advance proceeds like any other.
   - A projection that derives the legacy `kind` (`'conversion'`) or exposes `behavior` for `GET /lead-stages`, `GET /pipelines` and the lead wire: `kind` is `'conversion'` only when `stageActsAsOrcamento`; `behavior` is `effectiveStageBehavior(stage, modules)`. `type` is left as stored so the data is not rewritten.
   - The import planner/executor refusal of a lead "in the conversion etapa": refuse only when `stageActsAsOrcamento`; with `orcamentos` off the stage accepts imported leads like any normal stage.
   - The route passes `await getOrgModules(c)` into the service as a new TRAILING parameter `modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED` (default = today's FXL behaviour for any forgotten caller; the route-level test below catches a missed route). Services never read the context.
   - Stored data is never mutated by the toggle: the stage stays `type 'orcamento'` and a lead already converted keeps its `sale_id`.
4. `edition_capability` is the kept code for "your organization's modules do not include this". No new code.
5. Docs: CLAUDE.md paragraphs and a new reference file (below). Web rules in CLAUDE.md belong to slice 12b; do not edit them here.

## Steps (TDD)

1. Write `org-modules-routes.test.ts` (unit, stateful fake) and `org-modules-routes.integration.test.ts` (Tests). Run: RED (routes missing).
2. Add the two routes and the bootstrap key in `routes.ts` (code below). Unit test GREEN.
3. Add the stage-rule call sites (Design 3). Integration test GREEN (3 consecutive runs).
4. Add the two entries to `OPEN` in `edition-gate-map.test.ts`.
5. Docs. Then whole API unit suite, integration for the affected seed tests, lint, type-check, guards.

### Code for `routes.ts`

```ts
import { CRM_MODULE_KEYS } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
import { getOrgModules } from '../org-modules/context.js';
import { loadOrgModules, updateOrgModules } from '../org-modules/service.js';

const OrgModulesPatchSchema = z
  .object({
    modules: z
      .object(Object.fromEntries(CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((key) => [key, z.boolean().optional()])))
      .strict()
      .refine((value) => Object.keys(value).length > 0, { message: 'at_least_one_module' }),
  })
  .strict();

function orgTokenModules(c: Context): readonly unknown[] | undefined {
  return c.get('hubAuth')?.entitlements?.modules;
}

salesOpsRouter.get('/org-modules', requireAdmin, async (c) => {
  const state = await loadOrgModules(c.get('orgId'), orgTokenModules(c));
  return c.json({ modules: state.modules, sources: state.sources });
});

salesOpsRouter.put('/org-modules', requireAdmin, async (c) => {
  const parsed = OrgModulesPatchSchema.safeParse(await c.req.json().catch(() => ({})));
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return c.json({ error: 'validation_error', issues: parsed.error.flatten() }, 400);
  }
  const state = await updateOrgModules(c.get('orgId'), orgTokenModules(c), parsed.data.modules);
  return c.json({ modules: state.modules, sources: state.sources });
});

// in GET /bootstrap, last line:
return c.json({ ...snapshot, orgModules: await getOrgModules(c) });
```

(`Context` is already imported in `routes.ts`; if not, `import type { Context } from 'hono'`.)

## Tests

`org-modules-routes.test.ts` (unit; `vi.mock('../service.js')` with a stateful in-memory map keyed by orgId: `loadOrgModules` backfills from the token on first call, `updateOrgModules` merges the patch with `source 'admin'`):
- `GET /org-modules and PUT /org-modules answer the admin body to a non-admin` (context `userRole = 'seller'`): 403 with `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY` for both.
- `PUT rejects: {}`, `{ modules: {} }`, `{ modules: { orcamentos: 'yes' } }`, `{ modules: { nope: true } }`, `{ modules: { orcamentos: true }, org: 'x' }` -> each `400 validation_error`, `updateOrgModules` not called.
- `PUT then a gated route flips without a redeploy`: org with token `['sales.edition.leads']`, admin: `GET /sales` -> 403 byte-exact; `PUT { modules: { orcamentos: true } }` -> 200 and `modules.orcamentos === true`, `sources.orcamentos === 'admin'`; next request `GET /sales` (fresh request, fresh context) -> not 403; `PUT { modules: { orcamentos: false } }` -> next `GET /sales` is 403 again. (The handler behind `/sales` is mocked to a stub route or the test mounts `requireCapability('proposals')` on a probe path.)
- `GET /bootstrap carries orgModules` is covered in the integration test (needs the real snapshot).

`org-modules-routes.integration.test.ts` (real `salesOpsRouter`, real DB, RLS live; harness copied from `apps/api/src/domains/import/__tests__/import-routes.integration.test.ts` `createTestApp`, but the hand-built middleware sets NO `orgModules` and sets `hubAuth = hubAuthContext({ accountId, workspaceId: orgId, entitlements: { modules: tokenModules } })` where `tokenModules` is a mutable test variable, `userRole = 'admin'`, `userRoles = ['admin','seller','finder']`). Cleanup in `afterAll` through an admin connection, FK-safe: `DELETE FROM sales_ops_org_modules`, then the phase 2 tables created for the stage test (`sales_ops_crm_lead_events`, `_notes`, `_followups`, `_field_values`, `sales_ops_crm_leads`, `sales_ops_pipeline_transitions`, `sales_ops_pipeline_stages`, `sales_ops_pipelines`, `sales_ops_field_definitions`), `sales_ops_clients`, `sales_ops_people`, and `audit_log WHERE actor_org_id = ...`, all `WHERE org_id = <org>`; if phase 2 left a shared cleanup helper (`grep -rln "sales_ops_crm_leads" apps/api/test apps/api/src`), reuse it instead. Tests:
1. `a Construbom-like org (leads token, no rows) gets exactly today's leads capabilities`: first request `GET /summary` -> 403 and body text exactly `{"error":"forbidden","code":"edition_capability"}`; then for each of `GET /sales`, `GET /products`, `GET /areas`, `GET /funcoes`, `GET /history`, `PUT /settings` (body `{}`) -> byte-exact 403; for `GET /clients`, `GET /import/template` -> status 200; `GET /people`, `GET /bootstrap` -> 200. Admin read: 7 rows, all `token_backfill`, enabled keys `['clientes','importacao']`.
2. `the token no longer decides after the first request`: switch `tokenModules` to `[]` (a full token) on the same org: `GET /summary` is STILL 403.
3. `a full org (token [], no rows) is unchanged`: `GET /summary`, `/sales`, `/products`, `/areas`, `/funcoes`, `/history`, `/clients` all 200 (not 403); 7 rows all enabled.
4. `toggling a flag through the admin route changes the 403 without a redeploy`: Construbom-like org; `PUT /org-modules { modules: { orcamentos: true } }` -> 200; `GET /summary` -> 200; `GET /sales` -> 200; `PUT { modules: { orcamentos: false } }` -> `GET /summary` 403 again; `PUT { modules: { catalogo: true } }` -> `GET /products` 200 and `GET /history` still 403.
5. `ten concurrent first requests on a Construbom-like org: all 403, exactly 7 rows`: `Promise.all` of 10 `GET /summary` on a fresh org -> every status 403, no 500; admin `count(*)` is 7.
6. `GET /bootstrap exposes the org's modules`: the body has `orgModules` deep-equal to `modulesForEdition('leads')` for the leads org and all-true for the full org; existing keys (`sales`, `products`, `clients`, `people`, `settings`) still present.
7. `GET /org-modules is admin only and not capability gated`: with `userRole = 'seller'` -> 403 `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY`; with admin on the Construbom-like org (every capability but clients/import off) -> 200.
8. `an orcamento stage acts only with the orcamentos module` (uses the phase 2 contract routes): `GET /pipelines` (this also seeds the default pipeline), find a transition whose target stage has `type === 'orcamento'` and take its `fromStageId`; `POST /leads` with `{ contactName: 'Lead Teste', contactPhone: '11999990000', stageId: <fromStageId> }` (create a client or seller only if the phase 2 schema demands it); then
   - orcamentos OFF (leads token): `POST /leads/:id/advance { toStageId: <orcamentoStageId>, nextStep: 'Enviar proposta', dueOn: '<today SP>' }` -> 200, and `GET /lead-stages` shows that stage with `kind: 'normal'` and `behavior: 'none'`;
   - `PUT /org-modules { modules: { orcamentos: true } }`, create a second lead the same way: the same advance -> `409 { error: 'sale_required_for_conversion' }`, and `GET /lead-stages` shows `kind: 'conversion'`, `behavior: 'opens_orcamentos'`.
   If phase 2 named a route or field differently, follow the phase 2 plan's names and keep the two assertions.

`edition-gate-map.test.ts`: append to `OPEN`:
```ts
  r('salesOps', 'GET', '/org-modules', `${SO}/org-modules`),
  r('salesOps', 'PUT', '/org-modules', `${SO}/org-modules`),
```
The existing OPEN loop (`not edition-gated for a leads-edition owner`) then covers them (PUT with body `{}` answers 400 before any store call; GET reaches the mocked loader).

## Docs (one sentence per line)

`CLAUDE.md` (edit ONLY these paragraphs; line numbers as of this plan, locate by their first words):
- Auth Model, bullet starting `- Baseline access is the boolean`: replace the final clause `, plus the Sales edition, resolved ONLY in \`applyHubAuthContext\` into \`c.get('salesEdition')\`.` by `. The token's \`modules\` are read for the product surface by exactly one place, \`loadOrgModules\` (\`apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/service.ts\`), as a one-time backfill into \`sales_ops_org_modules\`; \`applyHubAuthContext\` resolves no edition.`
- Auth Model, bullet starting `- \`requireCapability\``: replace with: `- \`requireCapability\` (\`apps/api/src/middleware/require-capability.ts\`) is a CAPABILITY gate behind the access gate, never an access gate: \`403 {"error":"forbidden","code":"edition_capability"}\` (the code is kept; it means the organization's modules do not include the capability). It reads the org's modules from \`sales_ops_org_modules\` through \`getOrgModules\` (one query per request, memoized on the context, a store failure throws and never reads as "all enabled"), maps capability to modules with \`CAPABILITY_MODULES\` (\`@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model\`), a context that never loaded modules and has no Hub context is all modules, and gates are registered above every route of their router. Oracles: \`apps/api/src/middleware/__tests__/edition-gate-map.test.ts\` and \`apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/__tests__/org-modules-routes.integration.test.ts\`.`
- Edição Leads, the two bullets `- The edition is DERIVED per request from the verified token ...` (three physical lines) and `- API: \`c.get('salesEdition')\` is set only in ...`: replace with three bullets: (1) `The product surface is per-ORGANIZATION modules in \`sales_ops_org_modules\` (\`orcamentos|comissoes|financeiro|finders|catalogo|importacao|clientes\`), not an edition in the token.` plus `An org with NO module row is backfilled once, in a transaction with an advisory lock and ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, from \`modulesForEdition(resolveSalesEdition(entitlements.modules))\` with \`source = 'token_backfill'\`; after that the token decides nothing. \`resolveSalesEdition\` and \`SALES_EDITION_LEADS_MODULE\` survive for that backfill only.` (2) `An admin toggles a flag without a redeploy through \`GET/PUT /api/v1/sales-ops/org-modules\` (\`requireAdmin\`, not capability gated); \`GET /bootstrap\` carries \`orgModules\`.` (3) `\`c.get('salesEdition')\` no longer exists: \`getSalesEdition(c)\` is the PROJECTION (\`orcamentos\` off => \`leads\`), awaited in the route and passed to services as an explicit argument; services never read the Hono context. An \`orcamento\` stage opens the wizard / answers \`sale_required_for_conversion\` only while \`orcamentos\` is on (\`stageActsAsOrcamento\`); with it off it is a normal stage (\`kind: 'normal'\`, \`behavior: 'none'\` in the projections, the stored \`type\` is untouched).`
- Edição Leads, the bullet starting `- The leads edition grants exactly two capabilities`: replace `The leads edition grants exactly two capabilities, \`clients\` and \`import\` (\`LEADS_CAPABILITIES\` in \`sales-edition.ts\`)` by `An org with only the \`clientes\` and \`importacao\` modules (the Construbom backfill, \`LEADS_CAPABILITIES\` in \`sales-edition.ts\`) is granted exactly the capabilities \`clients\` and \`import\``; keep the rest of the sentence.

`nexo/knowledge/reference/auth-model.md`: rewrite the two bullets at lines ~19-22 (the `applyHubAuthContext ... resolveSalesEdition` sentence and the `requireCapability` sentence) to the new rule in the same words as CLAUDE.md.

# 12a - Navigation by module (phase 3, web, part 1)

## Context

- `apps/web/src/sales-ops/navigation.ts` (380 lines before phase 2): full lists `tacticalTeam`, `operational`, `cadastros`, `meusDadosSeller`, `meusDadosFinder`; parallel lists `leadsEditionOperational`, `leadsEditionCadastros`, `leadsEditionMeusDadosSeller`, `leadsEditionWorkspaces`; the functions `getSalesOpsWorkspaces`, `getVisibleWorkspaces`, `canSettleInWorkspace`, `getSalesOpsNavigation`, `getDefaultSalesOpsRoute`, `resolveSalesOpsRoute`, `workspaceForView`, each with a trailing `edition: SalesEdition = 'full'`.
