## Design (decided)

1. New `apps/web/src/sales-ops/org-modules.ts` (pure): `hasModule`, `hasEveryModule`, `isCompactProfile` (= `orcamentos` off), `modulesHintFromEdition(edition)`, `modulesCacheKey(modules)`. The hint turns the token-derived `profile.edition` into a module set (`'leads'` => `modulesForEdition('leads')`, otherwise `ALL_MODULES_ENABLED`); slice 12b replaces it by the real modules from the bootstrap once loaded.
2. ONE catalogue. Every entry is `{ id, label, icon, requires: CrmModuleKey[], compactLabel?, compactFirst? }`. The entry lists keep today's FULL order and labels. An entry is visible iff `hasEveryModule(modules, entry.requires)`. In the compact profile (`orcamentos` off) an entry shows `compactLabel` when set, and `compactFirst` entries are stably moved to the front. Nothing else varies by edition inside the lists.
3. The `requires` table (catalogue, workspace -> id -> requires, compactLabel, compactFirst):

   | Workspace | id | label | requires | compactLabel | compactFirst |
   | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
   | tatico | dashboard | Visão geral | orcamentos | | |
   | operacional | vendas | Propostas | orcamentos | | |
   | operacional | comissoes | Comissões | comissoes | | |
   | operacional | leads | Prospecção | (none) | | |
   | operacional | funil | Funil | (none) | | |
   | cadastros | produtos | Produtos & Serviços | catalogo | | |
   | cadastros | areas | Áreas | catalogo | | |
   | cadastros | clientes | Clientes | clientes | | |
   | cadastros | pessoas | Pessoas | (none) | Vendedores | yes |
   | cadastros | funcoes | Funções | catalogo | | |
   | cadastros | etapas | Etapas do funil | (none) | | |
   | cadastros | importacao | Importação | importacao | | |
   | cadastros | leads-excluidos | Leads excluídos | (none) | | |
   | cadastros | geral | Geral | financeiro | | |
   | meus-dados (seller) | vendedores | Meu painel | orcamentos | | |
   | meus-dados (seller) | comissoes | Comissões | comissoes | | |
   | meus-dados (seller) | leads | Minha prospecção | (none) | Prospecção | |
   | meus-dados (seller) | funil | Funil | (none) | | |
   | meus-dados (finder) | finders | Meu painel | finders | | |
   | meus-dados (finder) | vendas | Indicações | finders | | |

   Phase 2 entries: any entry for the Propostas submenu routes (they live under `operacional` and open propostas) requires `['orcamentos']`; the follow-ups entries (`operacional` and `meus-dados` seller) and the lead summary route require nothing. If phase 2 added a view id that is neither a Propostas submenu route nor follow-ups, STOP and report to the orchestrator (do not invent a rule). Where phase 2 inserted entries, keep their position in the full list; the compact result must equal today's leads list with the phase 2 non-orcamento entries inserted at the same relative position.
4. Workspace visibility, `getVisibleWorkspaces(roles, modules)`: candidates = `admin` => `tatico`, `operacional`, `cadastros`; plus `meus-dados` when (`seller` or `finder`) AND NOT (compact profile AND `admin`) - in the compact profile the admin wins outright, as the leads edition does today. Then keep a candidate only if `getSalesOpsNavigation(candidate, roles, modules).length > 0`. (This reproduces today: compact admin => `['operacional','cadastros']` because `tatico` is empty; compact finder-only => `[]` because both finder entries need `finders`; full unchanged because no list is ever empty.)
5. `getSalesOpsWorkspaces(modules)`: the unchanged `salesOpsWorkspaces` object when not compact, otherwise the same array with `operacional.description = 'Prospecção'` (built once at module load, as `leadsEditionWorkspaces` is today).
6. `canSettleInWorkspace(workspace, roles, modules)` = `hasModule(modules, 'orcamentos') && workspace === 'operacional' && roles.includes('admin')`.
7. `getSalesOpsNavigation` memoizes by `workspace + roles-flags + modulesCacheKey` so a repeated call returns the same array identity (the shell calls it every render). `meus-dados` = seller entries when `seller`, then finder entries when `finder`, each filtered.
8. `getDefaultSalesOpsRoute`, `resolveSalesOpsRoute`, `workspaceForView`: only rename the parameter `edition: SalesEdition = 'full'` to `modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED` and pass it through; no logic change.
9. The shell keeps a derived `edition` (projection) for its remaining chrome conditionals; in THIS slice it feeds the navigation functions with `modulesHintFromEdition(edition)` (so behaviour is unchanged); 12b feeds the real modules.
10. No admin toggle screen (decided, onboarding is out of scope): a toggle is the API call from 11c.

2. Write `navigation-modules.test.ts` (Tests 2-9). RED (module missing).
3. Create `org-modules.ts` (code below).
4. Rewrite the lists and functions in `navigation.ts` (code below). GREEN for both navigation tests.
5. Update the callers: `SalesOpsApp.tsx` (7 call sites, pass `modulesHintFromEdition(edition)` computed once: `const navModules = useMemo(() => modulesHintFromEdition(edition), [edition]);` next to `const edition = profile.edition;` and use `navModules` in every navigation call), `RoleGuard.tsx:65` (`getVisibleWorkspaces(roles, modulesHintFromEdition(edition))`), the two other test files.
6. Run the affected commands, lint, type-check.

### Code: `apps/web/src/sales-ops/org-modules.ts` (new)

```ts
import {
  ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
  CRM_MODULE_KEYS,
  modulesForEdition,
  type CrmModuleKey,
  type OrgModules,
} from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
import type { SalesEdition } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition';

export { ALL_MODULES_ENABLED };
export type { OrgModules };

export function hasModule(modules: OrgModules, key: CrmModuleKey): boolean {
  return modules[key] === true;
}

export function hasEveryModule(modules: OrgModules, keys: readonly CrmModuleKey[]): boolean {
  return keys.every((key) => hasModule(modules, key));
}

/** `orcamentos` off: the compact CRM profile (what the leads edition is today). */
export function isCompactProfile(modules: OrgModules): boolean {
  return !hasModule(modules, 'orcamentos');
}

/**
 * The module set implied by the token-derived `profile.edition`. A HINT used only until
 * the bootstrap delivers the organization's real modules (slice 12b), and as the fallback
 * for a hand-written test profile with no modules. Correct for every organization the
 * backfill has not been toggled away from.
 */
export function modulesHintFromEdition(edition: SalesEdition | undefined): OrgModules {
  return edition === 'leads' ? modulesForEdition('leads') : ALL_MODULES_ENABLED;
}

/** Stable key for memoizing per module set, for example `1011010`. */
export function modulesCacheKey(modules: OrgModules): string {
  return CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((key) => (hasModule(modules, key) ? '1' : '0')).join('');
}
```

Replace the arrays `tacticalTeam` ... `leadsEditionMeusDadosSeller` (and the big comment above the leads lists) by:

```ts
type NavEntry = SalesOpsNavigationItem & {
  /** Every module here must be enabled for the entry to show. Empty = always. */
  requires: readonly CrmModuleKey[];
  /** Label in the compact profile (`orcamentos` off). */
  compactLabel?: string;
  /** Moved to the front, stably, in the compact profile (its landing entry). */
  compactFirst?: true;
};

/*
  THE navigation catalogue. One list per workspace, in the FULL order. Visibility is
  `requires` against the organization's modules; the only other variance is the compact
  profile (`orcamentos` off): `compactLabel` and `compactFirst`. APPENDED, never prepended:
  `getDefaultSalesOpsRoute` lands on `[0]` of the visible list. Oracles:
  navigation-edition.test.ts (equivalence with both former editions) and
  navigation-modules.test.ts.
*/
const tacticalTeam: NavEntry[] = [
  { id: 'dashboard', label: 'Visão geral', icon: BarChart3, requires: ['orcamentos'] },
];
const operational: NavEntry[] = [
  { id: 'vendas', label: 'Propostas', icon: BriefcaseBusiness, requires: ['orcamentos'] },
  { id: 'comissoes', label: 'Comissões', icon: BadgeDollarSign, requires: ['comissoes'] },
  { id: 'leads', label: 'Prospecção', icon: LayoutGrid, requires: [] },
  { id: 'funil', label: 'Funil', icon: LayoutGrid, requires: [] },
  // phase 2 entries stay here, in the position slice 10b gave them
];
const cadastros: NavEntry[] = [
  { id: 'produtos', label: 'Produtos & Serviços', icon: Database, requires: ['catalogo'] },
  { id: 'areas', label: 'Áreas', icon: Layers, requires: ['catalogo'] },
  { id: 'clientes', label: 'Clientes', icon: ContactRound, requires: ['clientes'] },
  { id: 'pessoas', label: 'Pessoas', icon: UsersRound, requires: [], compactLabel: 'Vendedores', compactFirst: true },
  { id: 'funcoes', label: 'Funções', icon: Tags, requires: ['catalogo'] },
  { id: 'etapas', label: 'Etapas do funil', icon: ListChecks, requires: [] },
  { id: 'importacao', label: 'Importação', icon: FileSpreadsheet, requires: ['importacao'] },
  { id: 'leads-excluidos', label: 'Leads excluídos', icon: Trash2, requires: [] },
  { id: 'geral', label: 'Geral', icon: Cog, requires: ['financeiro'] },
];
const meusDadosSeller: NavEntry[] = [
  { id: 'vendedores', label: 'Meu painel', icon: UsersRound, requires: ['orcamentos'] },
  { id: 'comissoes', label: 'Comissões', icon: BadgeDollarSign, requires: ['comissoes'] },
  { id: 'leads', label: 'Minha prospecção', icon: LayoutGrid, requires: [], compactLabel: 'Prospecção' },
  { id: 'funil', label: 'Funil', icon: LayoutGrid, requires: [] },
];
const meusDadosFinder: NavEntry[] = [
  { id: 'finders', label: 'Meu painel', icon: Search, requires: ['finders'] },
  { id: 'vendas', label: 'Indicações', icon: BriefcaseBusiness, requires: ['finders'] },
];

function visibleEntries(entries: readonly NavEntry[], modules: OrgModules): SalesOpsNavigationItem[] {
  const compact = isCompactProfile(modules);
  const shown = entries.filter((entry) => hasEveryModule(modules, entry.requires));
  const ordered = compact
    ? [...shown.filter((entry) => entry.compactFirst), ...shown.filter((entry) => !entry.compactFirst)]
    : shown;
  return ordered.map(({ id, label, icon, compactLabel }) => ({
    id,
    icon,
    label: compact && compactLabel ? compactLabel : label,
  }));
}

const navigationCache = new Map<string, SalesOpsNavigationItem[]>();
```

`salesOpsWorkspaces` stays; replace `leadsEditionWorkspaces` by `compactWorkspaces` (same `.map` body) and the functions:

```ts
export function getSalesOpsWorkspaces(modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED) {
  return isCompactProfile(modules) ? compactWorkspaces : salesOpsWorkspaces;
}

export function getSalesOpsNavigation(
  workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace,
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): SalesOpsNavigationItem[] {
  const seller = roles.includes('seller');
  const finder = roles.includes('finder');
  const key = `${workspace}|${workspace === 'meus-dados' ? `${seller ? 1 : 0}${finder ? 1 : 0}` : '-'}|${modulesCacheKey(modules)}`;
  const cached = navigationCache.get(key);
  if (cached) return cached;
  let items: SalesOpsNavigationItem[];
  switch (workspace) {
    case 'tatico':
      items = visibleEntries(tacticalTeam, modules);
      break;
    case 'operacional':
      items = visibleEntries(operational, modules);
      break;
    case 'cadastros':
      items = visibleEntries(cadastros, modules);
      break;
    case 'meus-dados':
      items = [
        ...(seller ? visibleEntries(meusDadosSeller, modules) : []),
        ...(finder ? visibleEntries(meusDadosFinder, modules) : []),
      ];
      break;
  }
  navigationCache.set(key, items);
  return items;
}

export function getVisibleWorkspaces(
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): SalesOpsWorkspace[] {
  const roleSet = new Set(roles);
  const admin = roleSet.has('admin');
  const candidates: SalesOpsWorkspace[] = [];
  if (admin) candidates.push('tatico', 'operacional', 'cadastros');
  // In the compact profile the admin wins outright (the leads-edition rule).
  if ((roleSet.has('seller') || roleSet.has('finder')) && !(admin && isCompactProfile(modules))) {
    candidates.push('meus-dados');
  }
  return candidates.filter((id) => getSalesOpsNavigation(id, roles, modules).length > 0);
}

export function canSettleInWorkspace(
  workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace,
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): boolean {
  return hasModule(modules, 'orcamentos') && workspace === 'operacional' && roles.includes('admin');
}
```
(import `hasModule` too). Rename the `edition` parameter to `modules` in `getDefaultSalesOpsRoute`, `resolveSalesOpsRoute`, `workspaceForView` and in their inner calls, with type `OrgModules` and default `ALL_MODULES_ENABLED`. Delete the leftover comment about "THE LEADS EDITION" lists. Add `export const NAVIGATION_CATALOGUE = { tatico: tacticalTeam, operacional: operational, cadastros, meusDadosSeller, meusDadosFinder } as const;` for the coverage test.

   - `it('canSettleInWorkspace follows orcamentos')`: true for `(operacional, admin)` with all modules, false with `OFF('orcamentos')`.
   - `it('identity: getSalesOpsWorkspaces() with all modules is the exported salesOpsWorkspaces object')` and `it('repeated getSalesOpsNavigation calls return the same array')` (`toBe`).
   - `it('a finder-only operator sees meus-dados only while the finders module is on')`.
   - `it('every SalesOpsView is classified in the catalogue and every requires is a known module key')`: `const ALL_VIEWS: Record<SalesOpsView, true> = {...every id}` (so tsc fails when a view is added without a line here); assert each id appears in at least one `NAVIGATION_CATALOGUE` list and every `requires` entry satisfies `isCrmModuleKey`.

# 12b - Shell on the organization's modules, leaf projection, docs (phase 3, web part 2)

## Context

- After 12a the navigation functions take `OrgModules`; `SalesOpsApp.tsx` passes `navModules = modulesHintFromEdition(edition)` where `const edition = profile.edition;` (token hint, line ~1303).
- After 11c `GET /bootstrap` returns `orgModules` (additive). `SalesOpsBootstrap` is in `apps/web/src/sales-ops/types.ts:320`; the shell reads `bootstrapQuery = useSalesOpsBootstrap()` (line ~1307) and `const bootstrap = bootstrapQuery.data ?? emptyBootstrap;` (~1387).
- The route resolution runs BEFORE the bootstrap resolves (`resolveSalesOpsRoute(routeParams, profile.roles, ...)` ~line 1358) and the two `<Navigate>` early returns are at ~1788-1801, guarded by `const rolesAreAuthoritative = profile.isSignedIn;`.
- Leaf components read `useSalesEdition()` (`apps/web/src/auth/react.tsx:1035-1036`, `export const useSalesEdition = useHubSalesEdition;` ~1214); shell code reads `profile.edition`. Tests mock `@/auth/react` with a hand-written profile and `useSalesEdition: () => ...`.
- Shell chrome conditionals still on the edition: `SalesOpsApp.tsx` ~1738, 2000-2013 (`hasCapability(edition ?? 'full', ...)`), 2118, 2323, 2473; they keep working through the projection.
- Harness to copy for the new test: `apps/web/src/sales-ops/__tests__/leads-routing.test.tsx` lines 1-230 (mocks `@/auth/react`, `../hooks`, the lead containers, `@/components/ui/dialog`, `renderRoute`).

## Design (decided)

1. `SalesOpsBootstrap` gets `orgModules?: OrgModules` (optional: hand-written test fixtures have none).
2. In the shell:
   ```ts
   const bootstrapModules = isOrgModules(bootstrapQuery.data?.orgModules) ? bootstrapQuery.data.orgModules : null;
   const modules = bootstrapModules ?? modulesHintFromEdition(profile.edition);   // replaces navModules
   const edition = editionFromModules(modules);                                   // the projection, for the leaf chrome below
   const modulesSettled = bootstrapModules !== null || !bootstrapQuery.isLoading;
   ```
   `edition` is now defined AFTER `bootstrapQuery`; the hint uses `profile.edition` directly. Every navigation call uses `modules`. The `edition` constant keeps feeding the existing chrome conditionals unchanged.
3. Deep links: `rolesAreAuthoritative` becomes `profile.isSignedIn && modulesSettled`, and BOTH `<Navigate>` early returns use it. While the bootstrap is loading with no modules yet, the shell renders (as it does today during loading, the content area shows `LoadingPanel`) and rewrites nothing, so a valid deep link of an org whose modules differ from the token hint is not lost. A failed bootstrap (`isError`) is settled and falls back to the hint. Update the comment above `rolesAreAuthoritative` accordingly (keep the paragraph forbidding `null`/Skeleton while signed out).
4. Leaf projection: new `apps/web/src/auth/org-modules-context.ts` exports `OrgModulesContext = createContext<OrgModules | null>(null)`. `useHubSalesEdition()` in `react.tsx` becomes: `const modules = useContext(OrgModulesContext); const hub = useHubAuthContext().edition; return modules ? editionFromModules(modules) : hub;`. The shell's MAIN return (the one after the two `<Navigate>` returns) is wrapped in `<OrgModulesContext.Provider value={modules}>`. Hence a leaf under the shell reads the real organization's projection; outside it (and under every test mock) nothing changes.
5. `profile.edition` stays the token-derived HINT (documented as such): `RoleGuard`/`NoRoleGuard` run before any bootstrap, so a finder-only operator of an org toggled away from its token may see `/no-role` wrongly until a reload path exists. Accepted and documented; not worth a second fetch in the auth provider (token invariants).
6. API-side `kind`/`behavior` projections (11c) already hide an `orcamento` stage's wizard behaviour when `orcamentos` is off, so the board and Mover dialog need no module branch; this slice adds none. The proposta-only chrome keeps keying on `hasCapability(edition, ...)` through the projection.
7. No admin toggle screen (decided). An admin changes a module with `PUT /api/v1/sales-ops/org-modules` (11c).

## Tests

`org-modules-shell.test.tsx` (new; copy the mock block and `renderRoute` of `leads-routing.test.tsx`, but make the `../hooks` mock return a `bootstrapData` variable that the test sets, and `isLoading` a variable). Cases:
1. `the bootstrap's leads modules win over a full token hint`: profile edition `undefined` (hint full), roles `['admin','seller','finder']`, bootstrap `orgModules` = hand-written literal `{ orcamentos:false, comissoes:false, financeiro:false, finders:false, catalogo:false, importacao:true, clientes:true }`; `renderRoute('/tatico/dashboard', ...)` -> the router location ends at `/operacional/funil` (use the location probe the harness has), the sidebar has no `Propostas` item and has `Prospecção`.
2. `the bootstrap's full modules win over a leads token hint`: profile edition `'leads'`, bootstrap modules all true; `renderRoute('/operacional/vendas', ...)` stays at `/operacional/vendas` (not rewritten) and the sidebar shows `Propostas`.
3. `no redirect while the bootstrap is loading`: `isLoading: true`, `data: undefined`, profile edition `'leads'`, path `/operacional/vendas` -> location unchanged (still `/operacional/vendas`) while loading; then flip `isLoading: false` with full modules and re-render: still `/operacional/vendas`. (With the old code this URL would have been rewritten to `/operacional/funil` by the leads hint.)
4. `a failed bootstrap falls back to the hint`: `isError: true`, `isLoading: false`, edition `'leads'`, path `/operacional/vendas` -> redirected to `/operacional/funil`.
5. `a toggled flag shows its entry`: bootstrap modules = leads set with `comissoes: true`; the `operacional` sidebar lists `Comissões` before `Prospecção`.
6. `leaf components see the projection`: render a probe child inside the shell is not possible; instead unit-test the hook in `src/auth/__tests__/react.test.tsx`: `useSalesEdition()` under `<OrgModulesContext.Provider value={modulesForEdition('leads')}>` returns `'leads'` even when the token hint is `'full'`, and returns the hint with no provider (use the existing harness of that file; add one case).
7. Existing suites listed in `affected_tests` stay green untouched.

## Risks

- During the first bootstrap load of a never-toggled org the hint equals the real modules (the backfill reproduces the token), so nothing flashes. After a toggle that differs from the token the sidebar may show the hint's items for the duration of the first load; the content area shows `LoadingPanel`, and no URL is rewritten.
- `NoRoleGuard` uses the hint: documented limitation (Design 5).
- Wrapping the main return in a Provider adds no DOM; snapshot tests of the shell are unaffected.

diff --git a/apps/api/src/middleware/require-module-operator.ts b/apps/api/src/middleware/require-module-operator.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..bfec5f8
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/api/src/middleware/require-module-operator.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
+import type { MiddlewareHandler } from 'hono';
+import { env } from '../env.js';
+
+/**
+ * The 403 an organization's module write answers to an admin who is not a platform
+ * operator. Distinct from `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY` so a log line can tell "you lack
+ * the tenant admin role" from "your account may not change modules".
+ */
+export const MODULE_OPERATOR_REQUIRED_BODY = { error: 'forbidden', code: 'module_operator_required' } as const;
+
+/**
+ * The platform-operator allow-list, parsed from the validated env on every call. Empty
+ * or absent means nobody: default deny.
+ */
+export function moduleOperatorAccountIds(
+  raw: string | undefined = env.SALES_MODULE_OPERATOR_ACCOUNT_IDS,
+): ReadonlySet<string> {
+  return new Set(
+    (raw ?? '')
+      .split(',')
+      .map((id) => id.trim())
+      .filter((id) => id !== ''),
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * A tenant admin cannot enable a module for their own organization: a module write is a
+ * platform-operator action. It runs AFTER `requireAdmin`, and it reads only the verified
+ * account id (`c.get('userId')`), never the body and never the org.
+ */
+export const requireModuleOperator: MiddlewareHandler = async (c, next) => {
+  if (!moduleOperatorAccountIds().has(c.get('userId'))) {
+    return c.json(MODULE_OPERATOR_REQUIRED_BODY, 403);
+  }
+  return next();
+};
diff --git a/packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts b/packages/shared-utils/src/crm-model.ts

   backingColumn: 'client_id',
   position: 1,
 } as const;

+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Phase 3 (per-organization modules). Pure; shared by the API and the web.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+/** The module flags of one organization. Every key is present. */
+export type OrgModules = Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>;
+
+/** The fail-to-full value: a context that never resolved modules is the whole product. */
+export const ALL_MODULES_ENABLED: Readonly<OrgModules> = Object.freeze(
+  Object.fromEntries(CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((key) => [key, true])) as OrgModules,
+);
+
+/** A capability is granted iff EVERY module mapped to it is enabled. */
+export function modulesAllowCapability(modules: OrgModules, capability: SalesCapability): boolean {
+  return CAPABILITY_MODULES[capability].every((key) => modules[key] === true);
+}
+
+/**
+ * The legacy two-value projection. 'full' while `orcamentos` is on, 'leads' while it
+ * is off. It exists so every service argument and web conditional that still takes an
+ * edition keeps working; it never decides a capability (modulesAllowCapability does).
+ */
+export function editionFromModules(modules: OrgModules): SalesEdition {
+  return modules.orcamentos === true ? 'full' : 'leads';
+}
+
+/** True only for an object with every module key present as a boolean. */
+export function isOrgModules(value: unknown): value is OrgModules {
+  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
+  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
+  return CRM_MODULE_KEYS.every((key) => typeof record[key] === 'boolean');
+}
+
+/**
+ * An `orcamento` stage with the `opens_orcamentos` behaviour opens the proposta
+ * wizard / answers sale_required_for_conversion ONLY while the organization has the
+ * `orcamentos` module. With it off the stage is an ordinary stage.
+ */
+export function stageActsAsOrcamento(
+  stage: { type: string; behavior: string },
+  modules: OrgModules,
+): boolean {
+  return modules.orcamentos === true && stage.type === 'orcamento' && stage.behavior === 'opens_orcamentos';
+}
+
+/** The behaviour a stage really has for this organization (`none` when it cannot act). */
+export function effectiveStageBehavior(
+  stage: { type: string; behavior: string },
+  modules: OrgModules,
+): StageBehavior {
+  return stageActsAsOrcamento(stage, modules) ? 'opens_orcamentos' : 'none';
+}

import { effectiveStageBehavior, stageActsAsOrcamento, type OrgModules } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
import type { salesOpsPipelineStages } from '../../db/schema.js';

/**
 * The legacy etapa vocabulary, derived from the unified pipeline stage (phase 2 cutover).
 *
 * The web of today still speaks `kind` (`normal | conversion | lost`) and `isSystem`, so
 * `GET /lead-stages` and every other legacy projection derive them from the new columns and
 * never store them. Pure: imports only a type, no database, no clock.
 */
export type PipelineStageRow = typeof salesOpsPipelineStages.$inferSelect;

export type LegacyStageKind = 'normal' | 'conversion' | 'lost';

type KindInput = Pick<PipelineStageRow, 'type' | 'exitKind'>;

/** `orcamento` is the old conversion etapa, exit `perdido` the old lost one, everything else is normal. */
export function legacyStageKind(stage: KindInput): LegacyStageKind {
  if (stage.type === 'orcamento') return 'conversion';
  if (stage.exitKind === 'perdido') return 'lost';
  return 'normal';
}

/**
 * The old rule "every non-normal etapa is a system etapa": the conversion and the lost etapa
 * cannot be renamed or archived through `PATCH /lead-stages/:id`. Ganho and Pausado are ordinary
 * etapas the org owns.
 */
export function legacyStageIsSystem(stage: KindInput): boolean {
  return legacyStageKind(stage) !== 'normal';
}

/** `GET /lead-stages` row: every field of the old table row plus the additive pipeline fields. */
export type LeadStageView = {
  id: string;
  orgId: string;
  name: string;
  kind: LegacyStageKind;
  isSystem: boolean;
  position: number;
  status: string;
  archivedAt: Date | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date | null;
  pipelineId: string;
  type: string;
  behavior: string;
  exitKind: string | null;
  requiresReason: boolean;
};

export function toLeadStageView(row: PipelineStageRow): LeadStageView {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    orgId: row.orgId,
    name: row.name,
    kind: legacyStageKind(row),
    isSystem: legacyStageIsSystem(row),
    position: row.position,
    status: row.status,
    archivedAt: row.archivedAt,
    createdAt: row.createdAt,
    updatedAt: row.updatedAt,
    pipelineId: row.pipelineId,
    type: row.type,
    behavior: row.behavior,
    exitKind: row.exitKind,
    requiresReason: row.requiresReason,
  };
}

/**
 * The projection of an `orcamento` stage for ONE organization (per-org modules, phase 3).
 * A stage opens a proposta only while `orcamentos` is on; with it off the stage is an
 * ordinary stage in every projection. The STORED `type` and `behavior` are never rewritten,
 * so turning the module back on restores the stage exactly.
 */
export function legacyStageKindFor(stage: KindInput & { behavior: string }, modules: OrgModules): LegacyStageKind {
  const acts = stageActsAsOrcamento({ type: stage.type, behavior: stage.behavior }, modules);
  return legacyStageKind({ type: acts ? stage.type : 'normal', exitKind: stage.exitKind });
}

/** `GET /lead-stages` row as this organization's modules let the stage act. */
export function leadStageViewFor(view: LeadStageView, modules: OrgModules): LeadStageView {
  const kind = legacyStageKindFor(view, modules);
  return {
    ...view,
    kind,
    isSystem: kind !== 'normal',
    behavior: effectiveStageBehavior({ type: view.type, behavior: view.behavior }, modules),
  };
}
/**
 * CRM unificado: the shared vocabulary of the unified lead model (migration 0029).
 *
 * Pure: imports only sibling pure modules, reads no env, no clock, no locale.
 * Open-ended sets (behavior, field kind, reference target, module key, event kind,
 * source) are validated HERE and by zod, never by a database CHECK, because the
 * additive migration guard forbids DROP CONSTRAINT.
 */
import {
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES,
  type DefaultPipelineStage,
  type PipelineExitKind,
  type PipelineStageType,
} from './default-pipeline.js';
import { hasCapability, type SalesCapability, type SalesEdition } from './sales-edition.js';

export const CRM_STATUSES = ['active', 'archived'] as const;
export type CrmStatus = (typeof CRM_STATUSES)[number];

export const PIPELINE_STAGE_TYPES = ['normal', 'orcamento', 'saida'] as const satisfies readonly PipelineStageType[];
export const PIPELINE_EXIT_KINDS = ['ganho', 'perdido', 'pausado'] as const satisfies readonly PipelineExitKind[];

export const STAGE_BEHAVIORS = ['none', 'opens_orcamentos'] as const;
export type StageBehavior = (typeof STAGE_BEHAVIORS)[number];

export const FIELD_KINDS = ['text', 'number', 'money', 'date', 'options', 'reference'] as const;
export type FieldKind = (typeof FIELD_KINDS)[number];

export const FIELD_REFERENCE_TARGETS = ['cliente', 'contato', 'produto', 'servico', 'venda'] as const;
export type FieldReferenceTarget = (typeof FIELD_REFERENCE_TARGETS)[number];

export const LEAD_EVENT_KINDS = ['stage_changed'] as const;
export type LeadEventKind = (typeof LEAD_EVENT_KINDS)[number];

export const CRM_MODULE_KEYS = [
  'orcamentos',
  'comissoes',
  'financeiro',
  'finders',
  'catalogo',
  'importacao',
  'clientes',
] as const;
export type CrmModuleKey = (typeof CRM_MODULE_KEYS)[number];

export const CRM_MODULE_SOURCES = ['default', 'token_backfill', 'admin'] as const;
export type CrmModuleSource = (typeof CRM_MODULE_SOURCES)[number];

/**
 * Today's capability -> the module(s) that must ALL be enabled for it.
 * `financeiro` maps only `history` (Geral settings and the archive history) here;
 * phase 3 decides what else the financeiro module gates.
 */
export const CAPABILITY_MODULES: Readonly<Record<SalesCapability, readonly CrmModuleKey[]>> = {
  proposals: ['orcamentos'],
  commissions: ['comissoes'],
  catalog: ['catalogo'],
  clients: ['clientes'],
  import: ['importacao'],
  finders: ['finders'],
  history: ['financeiro'],
  leadFullFields: ['catalogo'],
};

/**
 * The module flags that reproduce an edition exactly: a module is enabled iff every
 * capability mapped to it is granted by the edition. full => all seven;
 * leads => exactly `clientes` and `importacao`. Used by the phase 3 one-time token backfill.
 */
export function modulesForEdition(edition: SalesEdition): Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean> {
  const result = {} as Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>;
  for (const key of CRM_MODULE_KEYS) {
    const mapped = (Object.keys(CAPABILITY_MODULES) as SalesCapability[]).filter((capability) =>
      CAPABILITY_MODULES[capability].includes(key),
    );
    result[key] = mapped.length > 0 && mapped.every((capability) => hasCapability(edition, capability));
  }
  return result;
}

export function isCrmModuleKey(value: unknown): value is CrmModuleKey {
  return typeof value === 'string' && (CRM_MODULE_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

/** The behavior a default stage is seeded with. */
export function defaultStageBehavior(stage: Pick<DefaultPipelineStage, 'type'>): StageBehavior {
  return stage.type === 'orcamento' ? 'opens_orcamentos' : 'none';
}

/** Only a perdido exit requires a reason. */
export function defaultStageRequiresReason(stage: Pick<DefaultPipelineStage, 'exitKind'>): boolean {
  return stage.exitKind === 'perdido';
}

export interface TransitionPlanStage {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly position: number;
  readonly type: PipelineStageType;
  readonly exitKind: PipelineExitKind | null;
  readonly status: CrmStatus;
}

/**
 * The transition rule migration 0030 applies in SQL, over the ACTIVE stages of one pipeline.
 * open = type normal|orcamento ordered by (position, name); exits = type saida.
 *   open[i] -> open[i+1]; open[i+1] -> open[i]; every open -> every exit;
 *   every exit with exitKind pausado -> every open.
 * Returns unique [fromId, toId] pairs in a stable order.
 */
export function planPipelineTransitions(stages: readonly TransitionPlanStage[]): [string, string][] {
  const active = stages.filter((stage) => stage.status === 'active');
  const byOrder = (a: TransitionPlanStage, b: TransitionPlanStage) =>
    a.position - b.position || (a.name < b.name ? -1 : a.name > b.name ? 1 : 0);
  const open = active.filter((stage) => stage.type !== 'saida').sort(byOrder);
  const exits = active.filter((stage) => stage.type === 'saida').sort(byOrder);
  const pairs = new Map<string, [string, string]>();
  const add = (from: string, to: string) => pairs.set(`${from}>${to}`, [from, to]);
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < open.length; i += 1) {
    add(open[i]!.id, open[i + 1]!.id);
    add(open[i + 1]!.id, open[i]!.id);
  }
  for (const from of open) for (const to of exits) add(from.id, to.id);
  for (const from of exits) if (from.exitKind === 'pausado') for (const to of open) add(from.id, to.id);
  return [...pairs.values()];
}

/** Key -> stage lookup of the default pipeline, for seeding and parity tests. */
export const DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGE_BY_KEY: ReadonlyMap<string, DefaultPipelineStage> = new Map(
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage.key, stage]),
);

  key: 'cliente',
  label: 'Cliente',
  kind: 'reference',
  referenceTarget: 'cliente',
  multiple: false,
  filterable: true,
  isSystem: true,
  removable: false,
  backingColumn: 'client_id',
  position: 1,
} as const;

 // Registered before POST /lead-stages for readability only; there is no
import {
  ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
  CRM_MODULE_KEYS,
  modulesForEdition,
  type CrmModuleKey,
  type OrgModules,
} from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
import type { SalesEdition } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sales-edition';

export { ALL_MODULES_ENABLED };
export type { OrgModules };

export function hasModule(modules: OrgModules, key: CrmModuleKey): boolean {
  return modules[key] === true;
}

export function hasEveryModule(modules: OrgModules, keys: readonly CrmModuleKey[]): boolean {
  return keys.every((key) => hasModule(modules, key));
}

/** `orcamentos` off: the compact CRM profile (what the leads edition is today). */
export function isCompactProfile(modules: OrgModules): boolean {
  return !hasModule(modules, 'orcamentos');
}

/**
 * The module set implied by the token-derived `profile.edition`. A HINT used only until
 * the bootstrap delivers the organization's real modules (slice 12b), and as the fallback
 * for a hand-written test profile with no modules. Correct for every organization the
 * backfill has not been toggled away from.
 */
export function modulesHintFromEdition(edition: SalesEdition | undefined): OrgModules {
  return edition === 'leads' ? modulesForEdition('leads') : ALL_MODULES_ENABLED;
}

/** Stable key for memoizing per module set, for example `1011010`. */
export function modulesCacheKey(modules: OrgModules): string {
  return CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((key) => (hasModule(modules, key) ? '1' : '0')).join('');
}

/**
 * The ONE Sales Ops route. The optional `saleId` segment keeps the list and the
 * proposta detail on the same route object, so moving between them never remounts
 * `SalesOpsApp` (filters, an open wizard and `convertedSales` survive).
 */
export const SALES_OPS_ROUTE_PATTERN = '/:workspace/:view/:saleId?';

export type SalesOpsRoute = Readonly<{
  workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace;
  view: SalesOpsView;
  /** The open proposta. Only ever present on the `vendas` view. */
  saleId?: string;
  /** The selected lead stage, from the `?etapa=` query, only on the leads view. */
  stageId?: string;
  /** The open lead summary. Only present on the leads view. */
  leadId?: string;
}>;

export type SalesOpsRouteParams = Readonly<{
  workspace?: string;
  view?: string;
  saleId?: string;
  /** The `?etapa=` query value, read by the caller. Only the leads view honours it. */
  etapa?: string;
}>;

export type SalesOpsRouteResolution = Readonly<{
  route: SalesOpsRoute;
  path: string;
  redirect: boolean;
}>;

type NavEntry = SalesOpsNavigationItem & {

export const salesOpsWorkspaces: Array<{
  id: SalesOpsWorkspace;
  label: string;
  description: string;
}> = [
  { id: 'tatico', label: 'Tático', description: 'Indicadores e painéis' },
  { id: 'operacional', label: 'Operacional', description: 'Propostas e conferência' },
  { id: 'cadastros', label: 'Cadastros', description: 'Pessoas, catálogo e regras' },
  { id: 'meus-dados', label: 'Meus dados', description: 'Painel e comissões pessoais' },
];

/** The compact profile's workspace catalogue: Operacional is described as Prospecção. Built once. */
const compactWorkspaces: ReadonlyArray<{
  id: SalesOpsWorkspace;
  label: string;
  description: string;
}> = salesOpsWorkspaces.map((item) =>
  item.id === 'operacional' ? { ...item, description: 'Prospecção' } : item,
);

/** The workspace catalogue for a module set. Every module on gives the SAME `salesOpsWorkspaces` object. */
export function getSalesOpsWorkspaces(
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): ReadonlyArray<{ id: SalesOpsWorkspace; label: string; description: string }> {
  return isCompactProfile(modules) ? compactWorkspaces : salesOpsWorkspaces;
}

function visibleEntries(entries: readonly NavEntry[], modules: OrgModules): SalesOpsNavigationItem[] {
  const compact = isCompactProfile(modules);
  const shown = entries.filter((entry) => hasEveryModule(modules, entry.requires));
  const ordered = compact
    ? [...shown.filter((entry) => entry.compactFirst), ...shown.filter((entry) => !entry.compactFirst)]
    : shown;
  return ordered.map(({ id, label, icon, compactLabel }) => ({
    id,
    icon,
    label: compact && compactLabel ? compactLabel : label,
  }));
}

/** Memo of the navigation lists: the shell calls these on every render and must get the same arrays back. */
const navigationCache = new Map<string, SalesOpsNavigationItem[]>();

export function getSalesOpsNavigation(
  workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace,
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): SalesOpsNavigationItem[] {
  const seller = roles.includes('seller');
  const finder = roles.includes('finder');
  const flags = workspace === 'meus-dados' ? `${seller ? 1 : 0}${finder ? 1 : 0}` : '-';
  const key = `${workspace}|${flags}|${modulesCacheKey(modules)}`;
  const cached = navigationCache.get(key);
  if (cached) return cached;
  let items: SalesOpsNavigationItem[];
  switch (workspace) {
    case 'tatico':
      items = visibleEntries(tacticalTeam, modules);
      break;
    case 'operacional':
      items = visibleEntries(operational, modules);
      break;
    case 'cadastros':
      items = visibleEntries(cadastros, modules);
      break;
    case 'meus-dados':
      items = [
        ...(seller ? visibleEntries(meusDadosSeller, modules) : []),
        ...(finder ? visibleEntries(meusDadosFinder, modules) : []),
      ];
      break;
  }
  navigationCache.set(key, items);
  return items;
}

export function getVisibleWorkspaces(
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): SalesOpsWorkspace[] {
  const roleSet = new Set(roles);
  const admin = roleSet.has('admin');
  const candidates: SalesOpsWorkspace[] = [];
  if (admin) candidates.push('tatico', 'operacional', 'cadastros');
  // In the compact profile the admin wins outright: the gestor is also a seller, and
  // the seller's own panel is not part of the compact product (the leads-edition rule).
  if ((roleSet.has('seller') || roleSet.has('finder')) && !(admin && isCompactProfile(modules))) {
    candidates.push('meus-dados');
  }
  // A workspace whose every entry is hidden by the modules is not offered.
  return candidates.filter((id) => getSalesOpsNavigation(id, roles, modules).length > 0);
}

/**
 * Baixa and estorno are admin actions of the `operacional` workspace only:
 * `meus-dados` reuses the same views and stays read-only for everyone. Both terms
 * are load-bearing; the admin one is belt and braces today because only an admin
 * can stand in `operacional`. Settling needs the `orcamentos` module (a proposta).
 */
export function canSettleInWorkspace(
  workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace,
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): boolean {
  return hasModule(modules, 'orcamentos') && workspace === 'operacional' && roles.includes('admin');
}

export function buildSalesOpsPath(route: SalesOpsRoute): string {
  const base = `/${route.workspace}/${route.view}`;
  if (route.view === 'leads') {
    if (route.leadId !== undefined) return `${base}/${encodeURIComponent(route.leadId)}`;
    return route.stageId === undefined ? base : `${base}?etapa=${encodeURIComponent(route.stageId)}`;
  }
  return route.saleId === undefined ? base : `${base}/${encodeURIComponent(route.saleId)}`;
}

export function buildLeadStagePath(workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace, stageId: string): string {
  return buildSalesOpsPath({ workspace, view: 'leads', stageId });
}

export function buildLeadSummaryPath(workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace, leadId: string): string {
  return buildSalesOpsPath({ workspace, view: 'leads', leadId });
}

export function buildLeadFunnelPath(workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace): string {
  return buildSalesOpsPath({ workspace, view: 'funil' });
}

/** The Finance `deepLinkPath` (audit 11.4): the team proposta detail. */
export function buildSaleDetailPath(saleId: string): string {
  return buildSalesOpsPath({ workspace: 'operacional', view: 'vendas', saleId });
}

export function getDefaultSalesOpsRoute(
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  preferredWorkspace?: SalesOpsWorkspace,
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): SalesOpsRoute {
  const visible = getVisibleWorkspaces(roles, modules);

  // Prospecção leads the sidebar, while Funil remains the landing page because
  // a bare leads URL has no stage to show and redirects there.
  const landingView = (target: SalesOpsWorkspace) => {
    const items = getSalesOpsNavigation(target, roles, modules);
    return items[0]?.id === 'leads' && items.some((item) => item.id === 'funil')
      ? 'funil'
      : items[0]?.id;
  };

  if (preferredWorkspace && visible.includes(preferredWorkspace)) {
    const preferredView = landingView(preferredWorkspace);
    if (preferredView) return { workspace: preferredWorkspace, view: preferredView };
  }

  const workspace = visible[0];
  if (workspace) {
    const view = landingView(workspace);
    if (view) return { workspace, view };
  }

  return { workspace: 'tatico', view: 'dashboard' };
}

/**
 * Bookmarked Cadastros URLs that lost their screen when Pessoas replaced the two
 * special-cased vendedor and finder cadastros. Both have a real successor, so the
 * URL is rewritten rather than dropped on the role default.
 */
const legacyCadastroViews: Readonly<Record<string, SalesOpsView>> = {
  vendedores: 'pessoas',
  finders: 'pessoas',
};

/**
 * Scoped to `cadastros` on purpose: `vendedores` and `finders` are still the two
 * live `meus-dados` view ids, so aliasing them workspace-wide would hijack the
 * seller and finder "Meu painel" routes.
 */
function aliasLegacyView(
  workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace,
  view: string | undefined,
): string | undefined {
  if (workspace !== 'cadastros' || view === undefined) return view;
  return legacyCadastroViews[view] ?? view;
}

export function resolveSalesOpsRoute(
  params: SalesOpsRouteParams,
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): SalesOpsRouteResolution {
  const workspace = getVisibleWorkspaces(roles, modules).find((id) => id === params.workspace);
  const requestedView = workspace ? aliasLegacyView(workspace, params.view) : params.view;
  const view = workspace
    ? getSalesOpsNavigation(workspace, roles, modules).find((item) => item.id === requestedView)?.id
    : undefined;

  if (workspace && view) {
    if (view === 'leads' && !params.saleId && !params.etapa) {
      const route: SalesOpsRoute = { workspace, view: 'funil' };
      return { route, path: buildSalesOpsPath(route), redirect: true };
    }
    // The third segment is the proposta id on `vendas` and the lead id on `leads`.
    // The `?etapa=` query is the stage selection, on `leads` only. Never write a key
    // as `undefined`: a present-but-undefined key trips `toStrictEqual`.
    const saleId = isSalesListView(view) && params.saleId ? params.saleId : undefined;
    const leadId = view === 'leads' && params.saleId ? params.saleId : undefined;
    const stageId = view === 'leads' && !leadId && params.etapa ? params.etapa : undefined;
    const route: SalesOpsRoute = {
      workspace,
      view,
      ...(saleId !== undefined ? { saleId } : {}),
      ...(leadId !== undefined ? { leadId } : {}),
      ...(stageId !== undefined ? { stageId } : {}),
    };
    const droppedSaleId = params.saleId !== undefined && saleId === undefined && leadId === undefined;
    const droppedEtapa = params.etapa !== undefined && stageId === undefined;
    // An aliased view or a dropped id differs from what the URL asked for, which is
    // exactly when the caller must rewrite the address bar. A canonical route stays `false`.
    return {
      route,
      path: buildSalesOpsPath(route),
      redirect: view !== params.view || droppedSaleId || droppedEtapa,
    };
  }

  const route = getDefaultSalesOpsRoute(roles, undefined, modules);
  return { route, path: buildSalesOpsPath(route), redirect: true };
}

export function workspaceForView(
  view: SalesOpsView,
  roles: readonly AppRole[],
  modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED,
): SalesOpsWorkspace {
  for (const workspace of getVisibleWorkspaces(roles, modules)) {
    if (getSalesOpsNavigation(workspace, roles, modules).some((item) => item.id === view)) {
      return workspace;
    }
  }
  return getDefaultSalesOpsRoute(roles, undefined, modules).workspace;
}

# 11c - Admin toggle route, bootstrap exposure, orcamento stage rule, API docs (phase 3)

## Context

- After 11b the gates and readers use `getOrgModules` / `getSalesEdition` (`apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/context.ts`), the loader/updater are in `service.ts` (11a).
- `salesOpsRouter` (`apps/api/src/domains/sales-ops/routes.ts`) registers the capability gates at the top (lines ~82-92), then `GET /bootstrap` (~line 94: `ensureLeadStagesForOrg` or, after phase 2, `ensureCrmDefaultsForOrg`, then `getSalesOpsSnapshot`, then `return c.json(snapshot)`).
- `requireAdmin` and `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY` are in `apps/api/src/middleware/require-admin.ts`.
- Phase 2 contract (`contracts/SEAM-CONTRACT-PHASE2.md`): `GET /pipelines`, `GET /lead-stages` (legacy `kind` derived: `type 'orcamento'` -> `'conversion'`, `exit_kind 'perdido'` -> `'lost'`, else `'normal'`; additive `type`, `behavior`, `exitKind`), `POST /leads/:id/advance` and `POST /leads/:id/move` answer `409 { error: 'sale_required_for_conversion' }` for a destination of type `orcamento` with behaviour `opens_orcamentos`. Today (pre phase 2) that refusal is `LeadInputError('sale_required_for_conversion')` at `apps/api/src/domains/sales-ops/leads/lead-service.ts:1310` and the import executor message at `apps/api/src/domains/import/executor.ts:91`.
- The zod import in `routes.ts` already exists (`z`).

## Design (decided)

1. Routes (in `routes.ts`, registered after the gates and before `/bootstrap`, so the gate comment "above every route" still holds):
   - `GET /org-modules` (`requireAdmin`): `{ modules: Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>, sources: Record<CrmModuleKey, 'default'|'token_backfill'|'admin'> }` from `loadOrgModules(orgId, tokenModules)`.
   - `PUT /org-modules` (`requireAdmin`): body `{ modules: { <key>?: boolean, ... } }`, strict (an unknown key, a non-boolean or an empty `modules` is `400 { error: 'validation_error', issues }`), answers `200` with the same body as GET after `updateOrgModules`. Dependencies between modules are NOT enforced (turning `orcamentos` off while `comissoes` stays on is allowed; the screens simply have nothing to attach to). No audit entry in this run (see Risks).
   - `tokenModules` is `c.get('hubAuth')?.entitlements?.modules`.
   - Neither route is capability gated; both are added to `edition-gate-map.test.ts` `OPEN`.
2. Bootstrap: `GET /bootstrap` returns `c.json({ ...snapshot, orgModules: await getOrgModules(c) })`. This is the web's source of truth for navigation (slice 12b). Additive; every existing key is untouched.
3. Orcamento stage rule. Pure helpers `stageActsAsOrcamento(stage, modules)` and `effectiveStageBehavior(stage, modules)` exist (11a). Apply them at EVERY decision site, found with:
   `grep -rn "opens_orcamentos\|'orcamento'\|'conversion'\|sale_required_for_conversion" apps/api/src --include='*.ts' | grep -v __tests__`
   Rules per hit kind:
   - A refusal or branch that makes an `orcamento`/`opens_orcamentos` destination require a sale (`sale_required_for_conversion`, move or advance): wrap the condition with `stageActsAsOrcamento(stage, modules)`. When it is false the stage is a normal stage: the move/advance proceeds like any other.
   - A projection that derives the legacy `kind` (`'conversion'`) or exposes `behavior` for `GET /lead-stages`, `GET /pipelines` and the lead wire: `kind` is `'conversion'` only when `stageActsAsOrcamento`; `behavior` is `effectiveStageBehavior(stage, modules)`. `type` is left as stored so the data is not rewritten.
   - The import planner/executor refusal of a lead "in the conversion etapa": refuse only when `stageActsAsOrcamento`; with `orcamentos` off the stage accepts imported leads like any normal stage.
   - The route passes `await getOrgModules(c)` into the service as a new TRAILING parameter `modules: OrgModules = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED` (default = today's FXL behaviour for any forgotten caller; the route-level test below catches a missed route). Services never read the context.
   - Stored data is never mutated by the toggle: the stage stays `type 'orcamento'` and a lead already converted keeps its `sale_id`.
4. `edition_capability` is the kept code for "your organization's modules do not include this". No new code.
5. Docs: CLAUDE.md paragraphs and a new reference file (below). Web rules in CLAUDE.md belong to slice 12b; do not edit them here.

## Steps (TDD)

1. Write `org-modules-routes.test.ts` (unit, stateful fake) and `org-modules-routes.integration.test.ts` (Tests). Run: RED (routes missing).
2. Add the two routes and the bootstrap key in `routes.ts` (code below). Unit test GREEN.
3. Add the stage-rule call sites (Design 3). Integration test GREEN (3 consecutive runs).
4. Add the two entries to `OPEN` in `edition-gate-map.test.ts`.
5. Docs. Then whole API unit suite, integration for the affected seed tests, lint, type-check, guards.

### Code for `routes.ts`

```ts
import { CRM_MODULE_KEYS } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model';
import { getOrgModules } from '../org-modules/context.js';
import { loadOrgModules, updateOrgModules } from '../org-modules/service.js';

const OrgModulesPatchSchema = z
  .object({
    modules: z
      .object(Object.fromEntries(CRM_MODULE_KEYS.map((key) => [key, z.boolean().optional()])))
      .strict()
      .refine((value) => Object.keys(value).length > 0, { message: 'at_least_one_module' }),
  })
  .strict();

function orgTokenModules(c: Context): readonly unknown[] | undefined {
  return c.get('hubAuth')?.entitlements?.modules;
}

salesOpsRouter.get('/org-modules', requireAdmin, async (c) => {
  const state = await loadOrgModules(c.get('orgId'), orgTokenModules(c));
  return c.json({ modules: state.modules, sources: state.sources });
});

salesOpsRouter.put('/org-modules', requireAdmin, async (c) => {
  const parsed = OrgModulesPatchSchema.safeParse(await c.req.json().catch(() => ({})));
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return c.json({ error: 'validation_error', issues: parsed.error.flatten() }, 400);
  }
  const state = await updateOrgModules(c.get('orgId'), orgTokenModules(c), parsed.data.modules);
  return c.json({ modules: state.modules, sources: state.sources });
});

// in GET /bootstrap, last line:
return c.json({ ...snapshot, orgModules: await getOrgModules(c) });
```

(`Context` is already imported in `routes.ts`; if not, `import type { Context } from 'hono'`.)

## Tests

`org-modules-routes.test.ts` (unit; `vi.mock('../service.js')` with a stateful in-memory map keyed by orgId: `loadOrgModules` backfills from the token on first call, `updateOrgModules` merges the patch with `source 'admin'`):
- `GET /org-modules and PUT /org-modules answer the admin body to a non-admin` (context `userRole = 'seller'`): 403 with `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY` for both.
- `PUT rejects: {}`, `{ modules: {} }`, `{ modules: { orcamentos: 'yes' } }`, `{ modules: { nope: true } }`, `{ modules: { orcamentos: true }, org: 'x' }` -> each `400 validation_error`, `updateOrgModules` not called.
- `PUT then a gated route flips without a redeploy`: org with token `['sales.edition.leads']`, admin: `GET /sales` -> 403 byte-exact; `PUT { modules: { orcamentos: true } }` -> 200 and `modules.orcamentos === true`, `sources.orcamentos === 'admin'`; next request `GET /sales` (fresh request, fresh context) -> not 403; `PUT { modules: { orcamentos: false } }` -> next `GET /sales` is 403 again. (The handler behind `/sales` is mocked to a stub route or the test mounts `requireCapability('proposals')` on a probe path.)
- `GET /bootstrap carries orgModules` is covered in the integration test (needs the real snapshot).

`org-modules-routes.integration.test.ts` (real `salesOpsRouter`, real DB, RLS live; harness copied from `apps/api/src/domains/import/__tests__/import-routes.integration.test.ts` `createTestApp`, but the hand-built middleware sets NO `orgModules` and sets `hubAuth = hubAuthContext({ accountId, workspaceId: orgId, entitlements: { modules: tokenModules } })` where `tokenModules` is a mutable test variable, `userRole = 'admin'`, `userRoles = ['admin','seller','finder']`). Cleanup in `afterAll` through an admin connection, FK-safe: `DELETE FROM sales_ops_org_modules`, then the phase 2 tables created for the stage test (`sales_ops_crm_lead_events`, `_notes`, `_followups`, `_field_values`, `sales_ops_crm_leads`, `sales_ops_pipeline_transitions`, `sales_ops_pipeline_stages`, `sales_ops_pipelines`, `sales_ops_field_definitions`), `sales_ops_clients`, `sales_ops_people`, and `audit_log WHERE actor_org_id = ...`, all `WHERE org_id = <org>`; if phase 2 left a shared cleanup helper (`grep -rln "sales_ops_crm_leads" apps/api/test apps/api/src`), reuse it instead. Tests:
1. `a Construbom-like org (leads token, no rows) gets exactly today's leads capabilities`: first request `GET /summary` -> 403 and body text exactly `{"error":"forbidden","code":"edition_capability"}`; then for each of `GET /sales`, `GET /products`, `GET /areas`, `GET /funcoes`, `GET /history`, `PUT /settings` (body `{}`) -> byte-exact 403; for `GET /clients`, `GET /import/template` -> status 200; `GET /people`, `GET /bootstrap` -> 200. Admin read: 7 rows, all `token_backfill`, enabled keys `['clientes','importacao']`.
2. `the token no longer decides after the first request`: switch `tokenModules` to `[]` (a full token) on the same org: `GET /summary` is STILL 403.
3. `a full org (token [], no rows) is unchanged`: `GET /summary`, `/sales`, `/products`, `/areas`, `/funcoes`, `/history`, `/clients` all 200 (not 403); 7 rows all enabled.
4. `toggling a flag through the admin route changes the 403 without a redeploy`: Construbom-like org; `PUT /org-modules { modules: { orcamentos: true } }` -> 200; `GET /summary` -> 200; `GET /sales` -> 200; `PUT { modules: { orcamentos: false } }` -> `GET /summary` 403 again; `PUT { modules: { catalogo: true } }` -> `GET /products` 200 and `GET /history` still 403.
5. `ten concurrent first requests on a Construbom-like org: all 403, exactly 7 rows`: `Promise.all` of 10 `GET /summary` on a fresh org -> every status 403, no 500; admin `count(*)` is 7.
6. `GET /bootstrap exposes the org's modules`: the body has `orgModules` deep-equal to `modulesForEdition('leads')` for the leads org and all-true for the full org; existing keys (`sales`, `products`, `clients`, `people`, `settings`) still present.
7. `GET /org-modules is admin only and not capability gated`: with `userRole = 'seller'` -> 403 `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY`; with admin on the Construbom-like org (every capability but clients/import off) -> 200.
8. `an orcamento stage acts only with the orcamentos module` (uses the phase 2 contract routes): `GET /pipelines` (this also seeds the default pipeline), find a transition whose target stage has `type === 'orcamento'` and take its `fromStageId`; `POST /leads` with `{ contactName: 'Lead Teste', contactPhone: '11999990000', stageId: <fromStageId> }` (create a client or seller only if the phase 2 schema demands it); then
   - orcamentos OFF (leads token): `POST /leads/:id/advance { toStageId: <orcamentoStageId>, nextStep: 'Enviar proposta', dueOn: '<today SP>' }` -> 200, and `GET /lead-stages` shows that stage with `kind: 'normal'` and `behavior: 'none'`;
   - `PUT /org-modules { modules: { orcamentos: true } }`, create a second lead the same way: the same advance -> `409 { error: 'sale_required_for_conversion' }`, and `GET /lead-stages` shows `kind: 'conversion'`, `behavior: 'opens_orcamentos'`.
   If phase 2 named a route or field differently, follow the phase 2 plan's names and keep the two assertions.

`edition-gate-map.test.ts`: append to `OPEN`:
```ts
  r('salesOps', 'GET', '/org-modules', `${SO}/org-modules`),
  r('salesOps', 'PUT', '/org-modules', `${SO}/org-modules`),
```
The existing OPEN loop (`not edition-gated for a leads-edition owner`) then covers them (PUT with body `{}` answers 400 before any store call; GET reaches the mocked loader).

## Docs (one sentence per line)

`CLAUDE.md` (edit ONLY these paragraphs; line numbers as of this plan, locate by their first words):
- Auth Model, bullet starting `- Baseline access is the boolean`: replace the final clause `, plus the Sales edition, resolved ONLY in \`applyHubAuthContext\` into \`c.get('salesEdition')\`.` by `. The token's \`modules\` are read for the product surface by exactly one place, \`loadOrgModules\` (\`apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/service.ts\`), as a one-time backfill into \`sales_ops_org_modules\`; \`applyHubAuthContext\` resolves no edition.`
- Auth Model, bullet starting `- \`requireCapability\``: replace with: `- \`requireCapability\` (\`apps/api/src/middleware/require-capability.ts\`) is a CAPABILITY gate behind the access gate, never an access gate: \`403 {"error":"forbidden","code":"edition_capability"}\` (the code is kept; it means the organization's modules do not include the capability). It reads the org's modules from \`sales_ops_org_modules\` through \`getOrgModules\` (one query per request, memoized on the context, a store failure throws and never reads as "all enabled"), maps capability to modules with \`CAPABILITY_MODULES\` (\`@fxl-sales/shared-utils/crm-model\`), a context that never loaded modules and has no Hub context is all modules, and gates are registered above every route of their router. Oracles: \`apps/api/src/middleware/__tests__/edition-gate-map.test.ts\` and \`apps/api/src/domains/org-modules/__tests__/org-modules-routes.integration.test.ts\`.`
- Edição Leads, the two bullets `- The edition is DERIVED per request from the verified token ...` (three physical lines) and `- API: \`c.get('salesEdition')\` is set only in ...`: replace with three bullets: (1) `The product surface is per-ORGANIZATION modules in \`sales_ops_org_modules\` (\`orcamentos|comissoes|financeiro|finders|catalogo|importacao|clientes\`), not an edition in the token.` plus `An org with NO module row is backfilled once, in a transaction with an advisory lock and ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, from \`modulesForEdition(resolveSalesEdition(entitlements.modules))\` with \`source = 'token_backfill'\`; after that the token decides nothing. \`resolveSalesEdition\` and \`SALES_EDITION_LEADS_MODULE\` survive for that backfill only.` (2) `An admin toggles a flag without a redeploy through \`GET/PUT /api/v1/sales-ops/org-modules\` (\`requireAdmin\`, not capability gated); \`GET /bootstrap\` carries \`orgModules\`.` (3) `\`c.get('salesEdition')\` no longer exists: \`getSalesEdition(c)\` is the PROJECTION (\`orcamentos\` off => \`leads\`), awaited in the route and passed to services as an explicit argument; services never read the Hono context. An \`orcamento\` stage opens the wizard / answers \`sale_required_for_conversion\` only while \`orcamentos\` is on (\`stageActsAsOrcamento\`); with it off it is a normal stage (\`kind: 'normal'\`, \`behavior: 'none'\` in the projections, the stored \`type\` is untouched).`
- Edição Leads, the bullet starting `- The leads edition grants exactly two capabilities`: replace `The leads edition grants exactly two capabilities, \`clients\` and \`import\` (\`LEADS_CAPABILITIES\` in \`sales-edition.ts\`)` by `An org with only the \`clientes\` and \`importacao\` modules (the Construbom backfill, \`LEADS_CAPABILITIES\` in \`sales-edition.ts\`) is granted exactly the capabilities \`clients\` and \`import\``; keep the rest of the sentence.

`nexo/knowledge/reference/auth-model.md`: rewrite the two bullets at lines ~19-22 (the `applyHubAuthContext ... resolveSalesEdition` sentence and the `requireCapability` sentence) to the new rule in the same words as CLAUDE.md.

`nexo/knowledge/reference/modulos-por-organizacao.md` (new; one sentence per line): sections `Tabela`, `Backfill do token (compat)`, `Capability para modulo` (the `CAPABILITY_MODULES` table), `Projecao de edicao`, `Rota de administracao`, `Etapa de orcamento`, `Dev-fake` (the two leads identities backfill into the LOCAL database on their first request), `Fora do escopo` (no module screen: onboarding is out of scope; a toggle is an admin API call), `Oraculos`. Slice 12b appends a `Web` section.

## Risks

Consolidated by the orchestrator from the planner's part plans (run budget: at most 24 slices).

## Plan-check addendum (orchestrator revision 3, binding)

Copied from the plan-check report `nexo/runs/20261021T011348Z-vendas-crm-unificado/agents/plan-check-fase34.md`. Where it conflicts with the body above, this addendum wins. Apply each fix in the part it names.

Orchestrator decision on D4: take the recommended option (requireAdmin plus the platform-operator allow-list `SALES_MODULE_OPERATOR_ACCOUNT_IDS`, empty by default, `403 module_operator_required`); register the new env name wherever the env guards (`no-legacy-env-names`, env example contract) require it.

#### D2 [MAJOR] 11b: `leadScope` becomes async but phase 2 adds un-awaited callers outside 11b's grep
Files: `parts/11b-api-modulos-cutover.md` Design 3 and step 2f.
`07b-api-pipeline-historico-followups.md:229-269` creates a new router file (`import { failureResponse, leadScope, validationResponse } from './leads/lead-routes.js'`, `listFollowups(..., leadScope(c))`, `markFollowupDone(..., leadScope(c))`), and `07b:1405-1559` exports `leadScope` and calls it synchronously in summary, advance, notes and follow-up handlers. These contain no `salesEdition` string, so 11b's `grep -rn "salesEdition"` does not find them; tsc would flag a Promise passed as `LeadScope`, but the plan promises a grep net, not a compiler net.
Replace 11b step 2f with:
> f. `domains/sales-ops/leads/lead-routes.ts`: make `leadScope` `async` (`export async function leadScope(c: Context): Promise<LeadScope>`, `const edition = await getSalesEdition(c);` then the same object) and `usesContactFields` async if it still exists. Then convert EVERY caller found by `grep -rn "leadScope(" apps/api/src | grep -v __tests__` (at least `lead-routes.ts` and the phase 2b CRM routes file that imports `leadScope`, e.g. the follow-ups/pipelines router of slice 07b): `await leadScope(c)`; `leadScope(c).isAdmin` becomes `(await leadScope(c)).isAdmin`. Add to Commands: `grep -rn "leadScope(c)" apps/api/src | grep -v "await leadScope(c)" | grep -v __tests__` must print nothing.
And in the Tests section add: any unit test of those routers that mounts them with a `hubAuth` and no `orgModules` presets `c.set('orgModules', ALL_MODULES_ENABLED)` (already in step 3, extend the sentence to the 07b router tests).


#### D3 [MAJOR] 11c: how the orcamentos flag reaches the services (single seam)
Files: `parts/11c-api-modulos-admin.md` Design 3 bullet "The route passes `await getOrgModules(c)` into the service as a new TRAILING parameter `modules ... = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED`".
Problem: `moveLead` already has a 6th parameter `options: MoveLeadOptions = {}` (07b), `advanceLead`, `getLeadSummary`, `listLeadStages`, `listPipelines` and the import executor each have different tails; a default of `ALL_MODULES_ENABLED` makes every forgotten caller fail OPEN to full behaviour (a Construbom lead stops at 409). Phase 2's contract keeps `leadScope(c)` as the single per-request seam (`07a-api-crm-cutover.md:111`).
Replace that bullet with:
> The lead services get the flag through the one seam they already receive: add `modules?: OrgModules` to `LeadScope` (next to `edition?`), filled by `leadScope(c)` from `await getOrgModules(c)` (11b already awaits there). `moveLead`, `advanceLead`, `getLeadSummary` and every lead read that projects `kind`/`behavior` use `stageActsAsOrcamento(stage, scope.modules ?? ALL_MODULES_ENABLED)`. Services that take no scope (`listLeadStages`, `listPipelines`, the import planner/executor, `mapping/catalog.ts` of slice 14) get a trailing `modules: OrgModules` parameter WITHOUT a default, so a forgotten caller is a type error, not a silent full-edition answer. Routes pass `await getOrgModules(c)`.
Also add the web side to the acceptance of D6 below.


#### D4 [MAJOR] 11c Risks / Design: `PUT /org-modules` open to any org admin (decision requested)
Files: `parts/11c-api-modulos-admin.md` Design 1, Tests (PUT cases), Risks "ORCHESTRATOR DECISION", Docs; `parts/11a` nothing.
Analysis: the request AC is only "toggle de flag muda navegacao e 403 sem redeploy". Nothing requires the tenant to toggle its own flags, and the request says onboarding/module configuration screens are OUT of scope (section 0, item 11). With `requireAdmin` alone, a Construbom org admin (Hub owner/admin of that workspace) can `PUT {orcamentos:true, comissoes:true, financeiro:true}` and unlock the paid/propostas surface, ledger and settlement routes for their own org. `hasAdminRole` is a tenant role, not a platform role; no platform-operator concept exists in the repo (`grep -ri "operator\|platform" apps/api/src` finds none in the auth path).
Recommendation (most conservative that still meets the AC): keep `GET /org-modules` as `requireAdmin` (read only, the bootstrap already exposes the same data); make `PUT /org-modules` require `requireAdmin` AND membership of a platform-operator allow-list that is EMPTY by default (default deny, fail closed). The AC test passes by configuring the allow-list for the test org's operator; production changes need an FXL operator account id in the env. A body or the token's org can never grant operator status.
Paste into 11c Design 1 (replace the `PUT /org-modules (requireAdmin)` bullet's first clause) :
> `PUT /org-modules` (`requireAdmin` then `requireModuleOperator`): `requireModuleOperator` (new file `apps/api/src/middleware/require-module-operator.ts`) answers `403 { error: 'forbidden', code: 'module_operator_required' }` unless `c.get('userId')` (the Hub account id) is listed in `env.SALES_MODULE_OPERATOR_ACCOUNT_IDS` (comma separated, trimmed, empty or absent = nobody). It reads the validated `env`, never `process.env`, and never anything from the body. `GET /org-modules` stays `requireAdmin` only. A tenant admin therefore cannot enable a module for their own organization.
Paste into 11c files_modified: `apps/api/src/env.ts` (new optional string `SALES_MODULE_OPERATOR_ACCOUNT_IDS`), `apps/api/src/middleware/require-module-operator.ts`, `apps/api/src/middleware/__tests__/require-module-operator.test.ts`, `apps/api/.env.dev.example` (a commented line `# SALES_MODULE_OPERATOR_ACCOUNT_IDS=` OUTSIDE the contract block read by `env-example-contract.test.ts`).
Paste into 11c Tests: (a) `PUT as an org admin who is not an operator -> 403 module_operator_required, updateOrgModules not called, rows unchanged`; (b) `PUT with an empty/absent allow-list -> 403 for everyone, admin or not`; (c) `GET stays 200 for the same admin`; (d) the existing "toggling a flag ... without a redeploy" integration test (test 4) and the stage test (test 8) run `PUT` as an operator (the harness sets `userId` to a listed account id via the mocked env); (e) a seller operator id still gets `ADMIN_ROLE_REQUIRED_BODY` (admin check first).
Paste into 11c Risks (replace the ORCHESTRATOR DECISION bullet):
> DECIDED: module writes are a platform-operator action, not a tenant-admin one. `PUT /org-modules` needs `requireAdmin` plus an id in `SALES_MODULE_OPERATOR_ACCOUNT_IDS` (default empty = nobody can toggle). Local dev-fake: set the variable to the fake identity's account id to toggle by hand. Staging/production: operators add their Hub account ids to the secret store; this is a configuration change, not a deploy of code. If modules become self-service later, that is a new product decision with an audit entry.
Paste into CLAUDE.md edit (11c Docs, the Edicao Leads bullet 2): replace `An admin toggles a flag without a redeploy through GET/PUT ... (requireAdmin, not capability gated)` with `An operator toggles a flag without a redeploy through PUT /api/v1/sales-ops/org-modules (requireAdmin plus the SALES_MODULE_OPERATOR_ACCOUNT_IDS allow-list, default deny, not capability gated); GET is requireAdmin only; GET /bootstrap carries orgModules.` Add `module_operator_required` to the OPEN-route reasoning of `edition-gate-map.test.ts` (PUT with body `{}` answers 403/400 before any store call, still never `edition_capability`).


#### m1 [MINOR] 11a/11b: backfill is first-request-wins on the token
The token consulted is whichever the org's first request carries. A staging org whose first call happens with an old session token lacking the module is backfilled `full` forever. Add one sentence to 11a Risks: "The backfill uses the first token seen; before releasing, list orgs and compare against the Hub entitlement of each (a read-only query on `sales_ops_org_modules` after the first week), and fix with `PUT /org-modules` as operator."


#### m3 [MINOR] 11c: bootstrap seed function name
11c Context says bootstrap calls `ensureLeadStagesForOrg` "or, after phase 2, `ensureCrmDefaultsForOrg`". After 07a the bootstrap still calls the facade `ensureLeadStagesForOrg` (07a keeps `stages-seed.ts` as a facade). Replace by "`ensureLeadStagesForOrg` (the 07a facade)" so the executor does not rewrite the call.

Copied from the plan-check report `nexo/runs/20261021T011348Z-vendas-crm-unificado/agents/plan-check-fase34.md`. Where it conflicts with the body above, this addendum wins. Apply each fix in the part it names.

#### D6 [MAJOR] 12b: the web re-derives `kind` from `type`, so 11c's projection does not reach the board
Files: `parts/12b-web-modulos-shell.md` Design item 6 ("the board and Mover dialog need no module branch"); upstream `parts/08a-web-crm-data-layer.md:77-80,94` (`LEGACY_KIND_BY_TYPE: orcamento -> 'conversion'`, `legacyKindOf(stage)` uses `type`, applied by `pipelineStagesAsBoardStages`) and `parts/10a` (`KIND_COLORS` keyed `orcamento` by `type`).
11c leaves `type` as stored and only changes `kind`/`behavior` in `GET /lead-stages` and `GET /pipelines`. The board consumes `GET /pipelines` and computes `kind` itself from `type`, so with orcamentos off the Proposta stage is still `kind: 'conversion'` in the web: drag/Mover opens the proposta wizard (the org has no `proposals` capability, `POST /sales` answers 403). 12b item 6 is false as written.
Fix in 12b (add to Design, Steps and files_modified; `apps/web/src/sales-ops/leads/` file that 08a created exporting `legacyKindOf`, find with `grep -rn "legacyKindOf" apps/web/src`):
> 6. The board must key the conversion kind on the EFFECTIVE `behavior`, not on `type`: change `legacyKindOf` to `(stage) => stage.exitKind ? LEGACY_KIND_BY_EXIT[stage.exitKind] : (stage.behavior === OPENS_ORCAMENTOS_BEHAVIOR ? 'conversion' : 'normal')` (lookup by object, no inline `kind ===`, the write-surface scan stays green) and drop `LEGACY_KIND_BY_TYPE`. `isSystem` stays `type !== 'normal'`. Colours may keep reading `type` (cosmetic). Add tests in the 08a data-layer test file: `an orcamento stage with behavior none (orcamentos off) maps to kind normal`, and in `org-modules-shell.test.tsx` `with orcamentos off the Proposta stage is a normal board stage and a drop on it calls the move, not onRequestConversion`.
Also require 11c to return the effective `behavior` from `GET /pipelines` (already in 11c Design 3 bullet 2; add `GET /pipelines` explicitly to the 11c test 8 assertions: `behavior: 'none'` with the module off, `'opens_orcamentos'` with it on).


#### D8 [MAJOR] 12a: make the phase 2 entries explicit in the catalogue and the equivalence oracle
File: `parts/12a-web-navegacao-por-modulo.md`, Design item 3 table and Tests.
Phase 2 actually appended (verified in `09e` and `10b`): `operacional`: `vendas-abertas` (Em aberto), `vendas-ganhas` (Vendidas), `vendas-encerradas` (Perdidas e canceladas) right after `vendas`, and `follow-ups` last, in the FULL list only for the first three; `follow-ups` in all four lists (`operacional` label `Follow-ups`, `meus-dados` seller label `Meus follow-ups`); the leads summary route `operacional|meus-dados/leads/:leadId` and `?etapa=<stageId>` (09a) are not nav entries. 12a's "names are decided by those plans" invites haiku to guess. Append these rows to the table:
| operacional | vendas-abertas | Em aberto | orcamentos | | |
| operacional | vendas-ganhas | Vendidas | orcamentos | | |
| operacional | vendas-encerradas | Perdidas e canceladas | orcamentos | | |
| operacional | follow-ups | Follow-ups | (none) | | |
| meus-dados (seller) | follow-ups | Meus follow-ups | (none) | | |
and state the resulting lists, which the equivalence oracle must pin literally: full `operacional` = `vendas, vendas-abertas, vendas-ganhas, vendas-encerradas, comissoes, leads, funil, follow-ups`; compact (`orcamentos` off, today's leads set) `operacional` = `leads, funil, follow-ups`; full seller `meus-dados` = `vendedores, comissoes, leads, funil, follow-ups`; compact seller `meus-dados` = `leads, funil, follow-ups`. These equal today's `navigation-edition.test.ts` tables after 09e/10b (the `*_LEADS` tables gain only `follow-ups`), so "modulesFor(edition)" keeps the oracle's meaning.
Add tests to `navigation-modules.test.ts`: (1) `vendas-abertas|vendas-ganhas|vendas-encerradas` are hidden together when only `orcamentos` is off and shown together when only `orcamentos` is on over the leads set; (2) `resolveSalesOpsRoute({workspace:'operacional', view:'vendas-ganhas', saleId:'x'}, ['admin'], leadsSet)` redirects to the role default and drops the id; with all modules it keeps `saleId` (the `isSalesListView` rule of 10b); (3) `resolveSalesOpsRoute({workspace:'operacional', view:'leads', saleId:'<lead uuid>'}, ['admin'], leadsSet)` and with the full set both resolve to the same non-redirect route (the lead summary), and a `?etapa=` query survives `buildSalesOpsPath` unchanged; (4) `follow-ups` stays visible when every module is off except the empty set `{}` all-false (still `leads, funil, follow-ups`). Add `resolveSalesOpsRoute` anchoring of `isSalesListView` (introduced by 10b) to the list of functions 12a must not change in logic.
Add to 12b test 3 ("no redirect while the bootstrap is loading") a second path `/operacional/leads/<lead uuid>?etapa=<id>` so the summary deep link is covered.

export function LeadSummaryContainer({
  leadId,

  onCreateClient,

}: LeadSummaryContainerProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const isAdmin = useAuthProfile().roles.includes('admin');
  const summaryQuery = useLeadSummary(leadId);
  const definitionsQuery = useFieldDefinitions();
  const saveLead = useSaveLead();
  const deleteLead = useDeleteLead();
