  const addNote = useAddLeadNote();
  const addFollowup = useAddLeadFollowup();
  const markDone = useMarkFollowupDone();
  const moveLead = useMoveLead(undefined);
  const pipelinesQuery = usePipelines();
  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [advanceOpen, setAdvanceOpen] = React.useState(false);
  // Stable per mount, as on the board: the day counters need days, not milliseconds.
  const today = React.useMemo(() => todayInSaoPaulo(), []);
  const now = React.useMemo(() => new Date(), []);

  const summary = summaryQuery.data;
  const lead = summary?.lead;
  const definitions = definitionsQuery.data ?? [];
  const backPath = buildSalesOpsPath({ workspace, view: 'funil' });
  const pipeline = React.useMemo(
    () => pickPipeline(pipelinesQuery.data ?? [], summaryQuery.data?.pipeline.id ?? null),
    [pipelinesQuery.data, summaryQuery.data?.pipeline.id],
  );
  // The destinations that open the proposta: the same set the board's conversion door uses.
  const opensConversionIds = React.useMemo(
    () => new Set((pipeline?.stages ?? []).filter((stage) => stageOpensOrcamentos(stage)).map((stage) => stage.id)),
    [pipeline],
  );

  function goBack() {
    if (isLeadSummaryOpenedInApp(location.state)) navigate(-1);
    else navigate(backPath, { replace: true });
  }

  if (summaryQuery.isPending) {
    return <Skeleton className="h-[320px] w-full" />;
  }

  if (summaryQuery.isError || !summary || !lead) {
    const notFound = isNotFoundError(summaryQuery.error);
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
        <p className={mutedStateClass}>{notFound ? LEAD_NOT_FOUND : LOAD_FAILED}</p>
        <button className={secondaryButtonClass} onClick={goBack} type="button">
          Voltar
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /*
    The inverse of an edit is only offered while the lead is still what this edit
    left: a fresh read must equal the response to the forward write (the board's rule).
  */
  async function requireUnchangedLead(expected: SalesOpsLead) {
    const fresh = await summaryQuery.refetch({ throwOnError: true });
    if (JSON.stringify(fresh.data?.lead) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) {
      throw new UndoConflictError(UNDO_CONFLICT);
    }
  }

  async function saveWithUndo(payload: LeadWritePayload) {
    const previous = lead!;
    const result = await saveLead.mutateAsync(payload);
    // The Cliente travels as clientId, never as a field entry, so it never counts as "added".
    const previousIds = new Set((previous.fields ?? []).map((f) => f.fieldId));
    const added = (result.lead.fields ?? [])
      .filter((f) => f.key !== CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY && !previousIds.has(f.fieldId))
      .map((f) => f.fieldId);
    // The inverse omits the contact keys, so it cannot take a contact away again: an edit that
    // gives a contactless lead its first contact is not undoable (the API keeps one contact).
    const addedFirstContact = !previous.contactPhone && !previous.contactEmail
      && (Boolean(result.lead.contactPhone) || Boolean(result.lead.contactEmail));
    if (addedFirstContact) return;
    undo.publish('Lead editado', async () => {
      await requireUnchangedLead(result.lead);
      return saveLead.mutateAsync(leadToWriteSeed(previous, added));
    });
  }

  /** Resolves `true` once the proposta exists and the lead has moved; `false` when the operator cancelled the wizard. */
  async function convert(stage: { id: string }): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!onRequestConversion) throw new Error('conversion_unavailable');
    const saleId = await onRequestConversion({ lead: lead!, toStageId: stage.id, toIndex: 0 });
    if (saleId === null) return false;
    await moveLead.mutateAsync({ leadId: lead!.id, toStageId: stage.id, toIndex: 0, saleId });
    return true;
  }

  async function confirmDelete(target: SalesOpsLead) {
    await deleteLead.mutateAsync(target.id);
    // The lead is gone and this page with it, so the restore notice would die here:
    // the lead stays restorable from Cadastros > Leads excluídos.
    navigate(backPath, { replace: true });
  }

  return (

      <LeadSummary
        definitions={definitions}
        now={now}
        notesSlot={(
          <>
            <NoteComposer onAdd={(body) => addNote.mutateAsync({ leadId: lead.id, body })} pending={addNote.isPending} />
            <FollowupComposer
   * silently dead in the common case: the click did nothing at all, which is a

   *
   * Navigation works for EVERY status, which is why it is one behaviour and not
   * a branch on the sale's status: two doors that look identical and behave
   * differently is how the operator learns not to trust either.
   *
   * It writes nothing and transitions nothing. The propostas screen remains the
   * only writer of `sale.status`.
   *
   * The link opens the proposta itself (`/operacional/vendas/:saleId`), still
   * writing nothing; closing it returns to the board through Back semantics.
   */
  function openSaleFromBoard(saleId: string) {
    navigate(buildSaleDetailPath(saleId), { state: SALE_DETAIL_OPENED_IN_APP });
  }
  /* BOARD-WRITE-FENCE:END conversion-handlers */

  useEffect(() => {
    if (modal?.kind !== 'person' || personModalMatchesRoute) return;

    const departedModal = modal;
    queueMicrotask(() => {
      if (mountedRef.current) {
        setModal((current) => (current === departedModal ? null : current));
      }

  }, [modal, personModalMatchesRoute]);

  const payableBrl = bootstrap.payables
    .filter((payable) => payable.status === 'open')
    .reduce((sum, payable) => sum + payable.amountBrl, 0);
  const roleLabel = roleSummaryLabel(profile.roles);
  const userName = profile.name ?? 'FXL';

  function setWorkspace(next: SalesOpsWorkspace) {
    setWorkspaceMenuOpen(false);
    setModal((current) => (current?.kind === 'person' ? null : current));

  function go(next: SalesOpsView) {

  function runHeaderAction() {

  }

  /*
    `leads` and `etapas` join `geral` at the head of the chain because both screens
    draw their own primary button (`Novo lead`, `Nova etapa`). Without naming them

import { testDatabaseUrls } from '../../src/db/__tests__/test-database-urls.js';
import { loadOrgModules, updateOrgModules } from '../../src/domains/org-modules/service.js';
import { withTenant } from '../../src/domains/sales-ops/leads/with-tenant.js';

const { appUrl: APP_DB_URL, adminUrl: ADMIN_DB_URL } = testDatabaseUrls();
const ADMIN_CONNECTION_OPTIONS = { connection: { 'app.fxl_admin': 'true' } } as const;
const LEADS_TOKEN = ['sales.edition.leads'];
const LEADS_SET = modulesForEdition('leads');
const FULL_SET = modulesForEdition('full');

function enabledKeys(modules: OrgModules): string[] {
  return CRM_MODULE_KEYS.filter((key) => modules[key]).sort();
}

describe('org modules: token backfill, concurrency, RLS and admin update', () => {
  let appClient: postgres.Sql;
  let adminClient: postgres.Sql;
  let db: ReturnType<typeof drizzle<typeof schema>>;
  const orgIds: string[] = [];

  function newOrg(label: string): string {
    const orgId = `org_mod_${label}_${Date.now()}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
    orgIds.push(orgId);
    return orgId;
  }

  function rowsOf(orgId: string) {
    return adminClient<{ module_key: string; enabled: boolean; source: string; updated_at: Date | null }[]>`
      SELECT module_key, enabled, source, updated_at FROM sales_ops_org_modules
      WHERE org_id = ${orgId} ORDER BY module_key`;
  }

  beforeAll(() => {
    appClient = postgres(APP_DB_URL, { max: 5 });
    adminClient = postgres(ADMIN_DB_URL, { max: 2, ...ADMIN_CONNECTION_OPTIONS });
    db = drizzle(appClient, { schema });
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    for (const orgId of orgIds) {
      await adminClient`DELETE FROM sales_ops_org_modules WHERE org_id = ${orgId}`;
    }
    await appClient.end();
    await adminClient.end();
  });

  it('a Construbom-like org (token module sales.edition.leads, no rows) backfills exactly the leads set', async () => {
    const orgId = newOrg('leads');
    const state = await loadOrgModules(orgId, LEADS_TOKEN, db);
    expect(state.modules).toEqual(LEADS_SET);
    const rows = await rowsOf(orgId);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(7);
    expect(rows.every((row) => row.source === 'token_backfill')).toBe(true);
    expect(rows.filter((row) => row.enabled).map((row) => row.module_key).sort()).toEqual(['clientes', 'importacao']);
  });

  it('a full org (token modules []) backfills all seven enabled', async () => {
    const orgId = newOrg('full');
    const state = await loadOrgModules(orgId, [], db);
    expect(state.modules).toEqual(FULL_SET);
    expect(await rowsOf(orgId)).toHaveLength(7);
  });

  it('token near-misses and unrelated add-ons backfill full; the exact module flips to leads', async () => {
    for (const tokenModules of [['sales.some-other-addon'], ['SALES.EDITION.LEADS'], ['sales.edition.leads '], undefined, null]) {
      const orgId = newOrg('nearmiss');
      expect(enabledKeys((await loadOrgModules(orgId, tokenModules, db)).modules)).toEqual(enabledKeys(FULL_SET));
    }
    const mixed = newOrg('mixed');
    expect((await loadOrgModules(mixed, ['sales.some-other-addon', 'sales.edition.leads'], db)).modules).toEqual(LEADS_SET);
  });

  it('after the backfill the token no longer decides', async () => {
    const orgId = newOrg('after');
    await loadOrgModules(orgId, LEADS_TOKEN, db);
    const second = await loadOrgModules(orgId, [], db);
    expect(second.modules).toEqual(LEADS_SET);
    const rows = await rowsOf(orgId);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(7);
    expect(rows.every((row) => row.updated_at === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('twelve concurrent first calls with mixed tokens write one coherent set', async () => {
    const orgId = newOrg('race');
    const results = await Promise.all(
      Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, index) => loadOrgModules(orgId, index % 2 === 0 ? LEADS_TOKEN : [], db)),
    );
    const first = results[0]!.modules;
    for (const result of results) expect(result.modules).toEqual(first);
    const rows = await rowsOf(orgId);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(7);
    expect(new Set(rows.map((row) => row.source))).toEqual(new Set(['token_backfill']));
    const enabledCount = rows.filter((row) => row.enabled).length;
    expect([2, 7]).toContain(enabledCount);
    expect(first).toEqual(enabledCount === 2 ? LEADS_SET : FULL_SET);
  });

  it('RLS isolates orgs', async () => {
    const orgA = newOrg('rls_a');
    const orgB = newOrg('rls_b');
    await loadOrgModules(orgA, LEADS_TOKEN, db);
    await loadOrgModules(orgB, [], db);
    const visibleToB = await withTenant(db, orgB, (tx) => tx.select().from(salesOpsOrgModules));
    expect(visibleToB).toHaveLength(7);
    expect(visibleToB.every((row) => row.orgId === orgB)).toBe(true);
    const leakedA = await withTenant(db, orgB, (tx) =>
      tx.select().from(salesOpsOrgModules).where(eq(salesOpsOrgModules.orgId, orgA)),
    );
    expect(leakedA).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('updateOrgModules turns a flag on as source admin and leaves the rest', async () => {
    const orgId = newOrg('admin');
    await loadOrgModules(orgId, LEADS_TOKEN, db);
    const state = await updateOrgModules(orgId, LEADS_TOKEN, { orcamentos: true }, db);
    expect(state.modules.orcamentos).toBe(true);
    expect(state.sources.orcamentos).toBe('admin');
    expect(state.sources.clientes).toBe('token_backfill');
    expect(state.modules.comissoes).toBe(false);
    const rows = await rowsOf(orgId);
    const touched = rows.filter((row) => row.updated_at !== null).map((row) => row.module_key);
    expect(touched).toEqual(['orcamentos']);
  });

  it('updateOrgModules on a never-seen org backfills first', async () => {
    const orgId = newOrg('admin_first');
    const state = await updateOrgModules(orgId, LEADS_TOKEN, { comissoes: true }, db);
    expect(state.modules).toEqual({ ...LEADS_SET, comissoes: true });
    expect(state.sources.comissoes).toBe('admin');
    expect(state.sources.clientes).toBe('token_backfill');
    expect(await rowsOf(orgId)).toHaveLength(7);
  });

  it('a partially populated org is not backfilled; a missing key reads false', async () => {
    const orgId = newOrg('partial');
    await adminClient`INSERT INTO sales_ops_org_modules (org_id, module_key, enabled, source)
      VALUES (${orgId}, 'orcamentos', true, 'admin')`;
    const state = await loadOrgModules(orgId, [], db);
    expect(enabledKeys(state.modules)).toEqual(['orcamentos']);
    const count = await adminClient`SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM sales_ops_org_modules WHERE org_id = ${orgId}`;
    expect(count[0]?.n).toBe(1);
  });

  it('an unknown module_key row is ignored and an unknown source reads default', async () => {
    const orgId = newOrg('unknown');
    await adminClient`INSERT INTO sales_ops_org_modules (org_id, module_key, enabled, source)
      VALUES (${orgId}, 'zzz', true, 'admin'), (${orgId}, 'clientes', true, 'weird')`;
    const state = await loadOrgModules(orgId, [], db);
    expect(Object.keys(state.modules)).not.toContain('zzz');
    expect(state.modules.clientes).toBe(true);
    expect(state.sources.clientes).toBe('default');
  });
});

+    'commissions',
+    'catalog',
+    'clients',
+    'import',
+    'finders',
+    'history',
+    'leadFullFields',

+    expect(Object.isFrozen(ALL_MODULES_ENABLED)).toBe(true);
+    expect(CRM_MODULE_KEYS.every((key) => ALL_MODULES_ENABLED[key] === true)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it.each(['full', 'leads'] as const)('modulesAllowCapability reproduces hasCapability for %s', (edition) => {
+    const modules = modulesForEdition(edition);
+    for (const capability of CAPABILITIES) {
+      expect(modulesAllowCapability(modules, capability), capability).toBe(hasCapability(edition, capability));
+    }
+  });

+    expect(editionFromModules(modulesForEdition('full'))).toBe('full');
+    expect(editionFromModules(modulesForEdition('leads'))).toBe('leads');
+  });
+
+  it('editionFromModules follows orcamentos alone', () => {
+    expect(editionFromModules({ ...ALL_MODULES_ENABLED, orcamentos: false })).toBe('leads');
+    expect(editionFromModules({ ...modulesForEdition('leads'), orcamentos: true })).toBe('full');
+  });
+
+  it('isOrgModules accepts a full record and rejects a missing key or a non-boolean', () => {
+    expect(isOrgModules(ALL_MODULES_ENABLED)).toBe(true);
+    const { catalogo: _missing, ...partial } = ALL_MODULES_ENABLED;
+    expect(isOrgModules(partial)).toBe(false);
+    expect(isOrgModules({ ...ALL_MODULES_ENABLED, clientes: 'yes' })).toBe(false);
+    expect(isOrgModules(null)).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it('stageActsAsOrcamento needs orcamentos on, type orcamento and behavior opens_orcamentos', () => {
+    const on: OrgModules = { ...ALL_MODULES_ENABLED };
+    const off: OrgModules = { ...ALL_MODULES_ENABLED, orcamentos: false };
+    for (const modules of [on, off]) {
+      for (const type of ['orcamento', 'normal']) {
+        for (const behavior of ['opens_orcamentos', 'none']) {
+          const stage = { type, behavior };
+          const expected = modules === on && type === 'orcamento' && behavior === 'opens_orcamentos';
+          expect(stageActsAsOrcamento(stage, modules)).toBe(expected);
+          expect(effectiveStageBehavior(stage, modules)).toBe(expected ? 'opens_orcamentos' : 'none');
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  });
